āṉai yāyiram amariṭai veṉṟa
māṉavaṉukku vakuppatu paraṇi  (ilak. vi. 839)
 eṉpaṉa yāṉaiyeṟi kōcattāṉaip paṟṟiyaṉa.
tāṉāl vilaṅkāl taṉittāl piṟaṉvaraittāl
yāṉai yeṟital iḷivaravāl - yāṉai
orukai yuṭaiya teṟivalō yāṉum
irukai cumantu vāḻvēṉ  (tol. puṟattiṇai. 5 urai)
eṉpatu āḷeṟi kōcattāṉaip paṟṟiyatu.
icceyyuṭkaḷellām tamiḻ maṟavaruṭ ciṟantāraik kuṟippa tallatu. oru vēṟṟunāṭṭu vakuppārai vitantō kiḷantō kuṟiyāmai kāṇka.
piṟkālac cōḻa pāṇṭiya koṅku nāṭukaḷil, paḻantamiḻk kōcariṉ vaḻiyiṉarāṉa kaikkōḷap paṭaimaṟavaruḷḷum talaivaruḷḷum pōrkkaḷattiṟ paṭṭavariṉ kuṭumpattārkku, irattakkāṇikkai yeṉṟum utirappaṭṭiyeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum mānilam aracarāl viṭappaṭṭamai āṅkāṅkuḷḷa kalveṭṭu kaḷ terivikkum.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟṟuṭaṉṟu mēlvariṉum āṟṟaluṭaṉ porum taṟu kaṇāṇmaip paṭaimaṟavarāṉa kaikkōḷar, 16ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkup piṉ (cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarāṉa) muttamiḻaraca marapum aṟṟuppōṉatiṉālum, pōrttoḻiṟ kiṭamiṉmai yālum, piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyamuṟaik kulappirivi ṉālum tolvaravun tōlum keṭṭu maṟam eṉṉum tiṟamum aṟavē iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. avar iṉṟu necavut toḻilai mēṟkoṇ ṭiruppiṉum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ teṉpākattil kaikkōḷar eṉṟum vaṭapākattil ceṅkuntar eṉṟum iṉṟum avarkku vaḻaṅkivarum peyarkaḷ. avariṉ muṉṉōr nilaimaiyait teṟṟeṉak kāṭṭāniṟkum. muṉṉark kaikkōḷar paṭai eṉpatu marapāka vaḻaṅkiyatu pōṉṟu, iṉṟu kaikkōḷar taṟi eṉpatu marapāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. kaikkōḷar peyar eṉṉun talaippil tivākaram kūṟum.
ceṅkuntap paṭaiyar cēṉaittalaivar
tantuvāyar kārukar kaikkōḷar
eṉṉum nūṟpā, kaikkōḷariṉ paṇṭai nilaimaiyaiyum iṟṟai nilaimaiyaiyum oruṅkē uṇarttum, tantuvāyār, kārukar eṉpaṉa necavupaṟṟi vanta peyarkaḷ, kuntam īṭṭi, ceṅkuntam kurutiyāṟ civanta īṭṭi.
paṇṭai vēḷiruḷḷum kōcaruḷḷum iruntatupōṉṟē, muṟaiyē avariṉ marapiṉarāṉa veḷḷāḷaruḷḷum kaikkōḷa ruḷḷum paṭaittalaivar iruntamaiyiṉ, iṉṟum avviru vakuppāriṉ vaḻiyiṉarum kulattārum. mutaliyār eṉṉum paṭṭattuṭaṉ aḻaikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉar. mutaliyār eṉpatu cēṉai mutaliyār allatu paṭai mutaliyār eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. mutaliyār iṅkut talaivar, vaṭakoṅkil vatiyum akampaṭiyar tamakku mutaliyār paṭṭam iṭṭukkoṇṭatu piṟkāla vaḻakku. tañcai māvaṭṭattil camaṇarkku vaḻaṅkum mutaliyār paṭṭam paṭait toṭarpiṉṟip potuvākat talaivar allatu periyōr eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatē.
kōcarum kaikkōḷarum oru vakuppārē eṉṟu mutaṉmutaṟ kūṟiyavar perumpulavar rā. irākavaiyaṅkārē. kōcar tamiḻarā vēṟṟu nāṭṭarā eṉpatupaṟṟi maṭṭum karuttuvēṟupāṭuḷḷatu. kaḻakak kālattuk kōcarum vēḷirum vaṭanāṭṭiṉ vevvēṟu pakutiyiṉiṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭup pukuntavar eṉpatu avar koḷkai. teṉṉāṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaiyāḷappeṟ ṟamaiyē. avar, aṅṅaṉam talaikīḻāṉa muṭipiṟku varakkāraṇam. teṉṉāṭṭu māntar vaṭanāṭṭāri ṉum muṟpaṭṭavar eṉpatai aṟintiruppiṉ, avar aṅṅaṉam koṇṭirār. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaṇṇapirāṉ nappiṉṉaiyai ēṟutaḻuvi maṇantatum, kāḷik kōṭṭattil (Calcutta) kāḷikku erumaik kaṭāvaik kāvu koṭuttatum, teṉṉāṭṭu (tamiḻnāṭṭu) vaḻakkaṅkaḷē. aṅ ṅaṉamē kācumīrattār kōca muṟaiyil cūḷiṭṭatum teṉ ṉāṭṭu vaḻakkamē. ātalāl, atupaṟṟik kōcarai vaṭanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vantavar eṉṟu koḷvatu varalāṟṟu malaivām.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil ulaka vaḻakkil kōḷar eṉṟum, ceyyuḷ allatu ilakkiya vaḻak kil kōcar eṉṟum, iṭaikkālattilum ikkālattilum iruvakai vaḻakkilum kaikkōḷar eṉṟum, ceṅkuntar eṉṟum, peyar vaḻaṅkappeṟṟavar oru vakuppārēyeṉṟum, teṉṉāṭṭut tūya tamiḻp paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷēyeṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
paṇṭaikkālattil, kollēṟu taḻuvi maṇanta iṭaiyarum kōḷarikkum añcāk kuṟavarum ākiya iru vakuppāriṉ vaḻi yiṉar āriyattāl iṉṟu tam muṉṉōriṉ taṟukaṇāṇmait tamiḻa maṟattai muṟṟum iḻantiruppatu pōṉṟē, kūṟṟuvaṉaic cīṟum kōcariṉ vaḻiyiṉarāṉa kaikkōḷar ceṅkuntarum iṉṟuḷḷaṉar eṉka.
piṉ kuṟippukaḷ :
 1. 	kōyaṉputtūr eṉpatu kōvaṉputtūr eṉpataṉ tiri pākavum irukkalām. kalveṭṭukaḷil uḷḷatu kōvaṉ puttūr eṉṉum vaṭivamē. vakaram yakaramākat tirital iyalpu.
 	e-ṭu : kōvil - kōyil.
 	iṉi, yarakam vakaramākat tiritalumuṇṭu.
 	e-ṭu : cēyaṭi - cēvaṭi.
 	itu. kōyaṉ - kōvaṉ eṉṉum tiripiṟkuc cārpākalām.
 2. 	muttamiḻ nāṭṭilum kōcar iruntaṉar. avaruṭ cilar tuḷu nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟiṉar. ataṉāl kōcarnāṭu tuḷu nāṭeṉap paṭṭatu. itu, cērar, koṅku eṉpatu pōṉṟatē.
 3. 	aṉṉi miñiliyiṉ tantai kaṇṇai kōcar orucilar piṭuṅkiya koṭumai paṟṟik kōcar ellāraiyum ayalār eṉṟu koḷḷamuṭiyātu. vāykkālil viḻunta māṅ kaṉiyait tiṉṟa ciṟumiyaik koṉṟa naṉṉaṉum tamiḻaṉē.
 4. 	ceṅkuntar kuṭippiṟanta oṭṭakkūttar pōṉṟa kaḻakakkālak kōcaruḷḷum tamiḻppulavar orucilar iruntaṉar.
 5.	iṟṟaik kaikkōḷarum ceṅkuntarum kaṭukaḷavēṉum ayaṉmai yuṭaiyavarallar; aṅṅaṉamē kaḻakakkālak kōcarum.
 6. 	mōriyap paṭaiyeṭuppiṟku muṉpē kōcar tamiḻakattirun taṉar. ātalāl, avar mōriyaroṭu vantavarallar. (teṉ moḻi, poṅkal malar 1960.)
kōṭṭam
ul - ulam = uruṭci. ulamvarutal - ulamarutal = cuḻalutal. ul- ula - ulavu - ulāvu. ulavutal = vaḷaital, cūḻtal, cuṟṟutal, tirital.
ul - kul - kula - kulavu.
kulavutal = 1. vaḷaital kulavuc ciṉaip pūkkoytu (puṟam. 11:4). 2. ulāvutal emataṉṉaiyai niṉaittē kulaviṉaṉō (civaraka. vicayai. 14).
kulavu = vaḷaivu. kulavuk koṭuñcilai (pu.ve. 1: 10)
kulavu - kulāvu. kulāvutal = vaḷaital. vaḷaittal. kulāvuñ cilaiyar (pu.ve. 4:3)
kulu - kuluttam = vaḷainta kāyiluḷḷa payaṟṟu vakai. (tivā.)
kul - kol - kōl = 1. (uruṇṭa) tiraṭci, kōṉiṟavaḷai yiṉārkku (cīvaka. 209). kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu (cilap. 26:121). 2. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa taṭi.
kōl - kōlam = pantupōṟ curuḷum muḷḷeli.
kōl - kōli = kuṇṭu vaṭivāṉa viḷiyāṭṭuk karuvi. ma. kōli, te. kōli (g), tu. kōḷi (g), marā. kōlī (g).
kul - kuṉ - kuṉi, kuṉital = 1. vaḷaital, kuṉivaḷar cilai (cīvaka. 486). 2. vaṇaṅkutal. (cūṭā).
kuṉ - kuṉuku = kuṉukutal = cirittu uṭal vaḷaital.
kuṉ - kūṉ = 1. vaḷaivu, kūṉirumpiṉiṟ kuṟaittu (naiṭata. nāṭṭup 10.) 2. uṭaṟkūṉal. (tivā). kūṉuṅ kuṟaḷum (puṟam. 28). 3. kūṉaṉ. (kūṉi). ciṟukuṟuṅ kūṉum (cilap. 27: 214).
kūṉ - kūṉi = vaḷainta ciṟṟiṟāl. kūṉikotti....... kok kirukkum paṇṇai (kuṟṟā. kuṟa. 94).
kūṉutal = 1. vaḷaital. 2. mutuku vaḷaintupōtal.
kūṉ - kūṉai - mūlaiyiṟ kūṉuḷḷa nīrccāl.
kulavu - kuravu - kuravai = ciṟappu nāṭkaḷiṟ kuṟiñci allatu mullai nila makaḷir eḻuvar allatu paṉṉiruvar vaṭṭamāka niṉṟu. pāṭiyāṭic cuṟṟivaruṅ kūttu... āycciyar kuravai. kuṉṟak kuravai. (cilap.)
kuravu - o.nō: L. curvo, E. curve, to bend.
kuravai - o.nō: Gk. choros, a dance in a ring.
E. “chorus, n. (L. chorus, from Gk. koros, a dance in a ring, a chorus). 1. In the Greek drama, (a) originally a company of dancers dancing in a ring accompanied by their own singing or that of others; a band of singers and dancers.”
E. “choir, n. (Written also quire, from O.Fr. choeur L. chorus, Gk. choros, a dance in a ring, a band) 1. a band of dancers. .... 2. a collection of singers, especially in divine service, in a church.....”  (The Imperial Dictionary of the English Language.)
kuravu - kuraku - kuraṅku, kuraṅkutal = vaḷaital. pāṭiṉāḷ ... ilaippoḻil kuraṅkiṉa (cīvaka. 657).
kuraṅku - 1. vaḷaivu. kuraṅkamai yuṭutta marampayi laṭukkattu (cilap. 10 : 157), 2. kokki.
kuraṅku - o.nō : E. crank.
kulu - kuru - kuruku = 1. vaḷaiyal, kaikuvi piṭittuk kurukaṇi ceṟitta (kallā. 42 : 22). 2. vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa nīrp paṟavai nārai. vāṉpaṟaik kuruki ṉeṭuvari poṟpa (patiṟṟup. 83:2). 3. nārai vaṭivotta kollulait turutti. ūtulaik kuruki ṉuyirttaṉar (cilap. 4:59) 4. ōtimam (aṉṉam). nīroḻiyap pāluṇ kurukiṟṟerintu (nālaṭi. 135). 5. aṉṟil, kuruku peyark kuṉṟam (maṇi. 5:13).
kuruku - o.nō: E. crane, OE. cran, OS., OHG. krano F. grue.
kuru - kuruḷ - kuruḷutal = curuḷutal. kuruṇṭa vārkuḻal (tiruvicai. tiruvāli. 1:3)
kuruḷ = 1. neṟṟi mayirccuruḷ. (w). 2. peṇṭir talai mayir. (piṅ.).
kuruḷ - o.nō: E.curl, MDu. krul, G. krol, LG., Du., E Fris, kurl.
kuru - kuṟu - kiṟu. kiṟukiṟuttal = 1. talai cuṟṟutal. 2. mayakkamaṭaital.
kiṟukiṟuvāṉam - ciṟuvar cuḻaṟṟi viḷaiyāṭum porivāṇam.
kuṟu - kuṟa - kuṟaṅku - kaṟaṅku.
kaṟaṅkutal = 1. cuḻalutal. pamparattu.... kaṟaṅkiya paṭiya (kantapu. tirunakarap. 28). 2. cūḻtal. kaṟaṅkiruḷ mālaikkum (tiruvaḷḷuvamālai, 34).
kaṟaṅkal = 1. cuḻaṟci. (piṅ.). 2. vaḷaitaṭi. (aka.ni.).
kaṟaṅku = 1. cuḻaṟci (tivā.). 2. kāṟṟāṭi. kāṉmuka mēṟṟa.... kaṟaṅkum (kallā. kaṇapati).
kaṟaṅku - o.nō: Gk. guros, ring, L. gyre, E. gyate, go in circle or spiral, revolve, whirl
kuṟu - kuṟaḷ - kuṟaṇṭu. kuṟaṇṭutal = 1. vaḷaital. 2. curuṇṭu koḷḷutal.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷam = 1. vaḷainta neṟṟi, tirukkuḷa muḷaitta kaṭṭāmarai (kallā. 31:9). 2. vella vuruṇṭai
kuḷam (vellam) - va. gula.
kuḷam - kuḷampu = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa vilaṅkup pātam.
 kuḷ - kuḷi - kuḷiyam = 1. uruṇṭai. (aka.ni.). 2. uruṇṭaiyāṉa maruntu māttirai. (piṅ.).
kuḷi - kuḷikai = 1. uruṇṭaiyāṉa maruntu māttirai. 2. poṉṉākka māttirai. kuḷikaiyiṭṭup poṉṉākkuvaṉ (tiruman. 2709).
kuḷikai - va. kuḷikā (gulika).
kuḷ - kuḷal - kuḻal.

kuḻal = 1. mayirk kuḻaṟci. kuḻaluṭaic cikaḻikai (cīvaka. 1092). 2. peṇṭir talaimayir curuṭṭi muṭikkappaṭukai. (tivā.).
kuḷiyam - kuḻiyam.
kuḻiyam = 1. vaḷaitaṭi. (w.). 2. naṟumaṇa vuṇṭai. (cilap. 14 : 171; arum.). 3. uruṭci. (tivā.)
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = 1. uruṇṭiruppatu allatu uruṇṭu kaṉat tiruppatu. 2. uruṇṭaiyāṉa niṟaikal. 3. uruṇṭaiyāṉa poṉ maṇi. poṉṉiṉ paṭṭaimēṟ kuṇṭu vaittu (S.I.I. ii. 182).
4. vilaṅkiṉ vitai. 5. veṭikuṇṭu. 6. uruṇṭai māmpaḻa vakai. 7. uruṇṭai kala vakai. 8. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa āṇ kutirai allatu āṇ kaḻutai. te. kuṇṭa. marā. kuṇṭa (g).

kuṇṭu - kuṇṭalam = 1. vaṭṭam 2. cuṉṉam, 3. vāṉam. (piṅ.). 4. āṭavar kātaṇi vaḷaiyam.
kuṇṭalam - va. kuṇṭala.
kuḷ - kuṇ - kuṇa - kuṇaku. kuṇakutal = vaḷaital.
kuṇaku - kuṇaṅku, kuṇaṅkutal = vaḷaital.

kuṇaku - kuṇaku - kuṇukku = 1. ciṟumiyar kātu vaḷaiyam. 2. mīṉvalai īyakuṇṭu.
kuṇukkutal = vaḷaittal.
kuṇalai = nāṇattāl uṭal vaḷaikai kūccamumāyc caṟṟē kuṇalaiyumāy (paṇaviṭu. 310).
kuṇa - kuṇam - kuṭam = 1. uruṇṭa tiraṭci. (W.). 2. vaṇṭik kuṭam. 3. nīrkkuṭam. 4. kumpa ōrai. (paṉṉirupā. 163). 5. kuṭatāṭi 6. kuṭakkūttu. nīṇila maḷantō ṉāṭiya kuṭamum (cilap. 6:55).
kuṭam - va. kaṭam (ghata).
kuṭakkam = vaḷaivu. kuṭakki = vaḷaivāṉatu.

kuṭam - kuṭantam = 1. mey vaḷaittuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭu. kuṭantam paṭṭuk koḻumalar citaṟi (tirumuru. 229). 2. nālviral muṭakkip peruviral niṟutti neñciṭai vaikkai. (tirumuru. pak. 47, aṭikkuṟippu.). 3. kuṭam. (piṅ.).
kuṭam - kuṭā = 1. vaḷaivu : kuṭāvaṭi yuḷiyamum (cilap. 25:50), 2. kuṭākkaṭal, vaḷaikuṭā, e-ṭu : maṉṉārkuṭā. virikuṭā, e-ṭu : vaṅkāḷakkuṭā, 3. mūlai (w.).
kuṭam - kuṭi = vaḷainta puruvam. (piṅ.).
kuḷ - kūḷ - kūḷi = vaḷainta paccai vāḻaippaḻam.
kuḷ - koḷ = vaḷainta payaṟṟaṅkāy. atiluḷḷa payaṟu.
koḷ - koṇpu - kompu = vilaṅkiṉ talaimēluḷḷa vaḷainta vuṟuppu, marakkiḷai, kōl, piḷḷai, peṇpiḷḷai, vaḷainta eḻuttu vari.
kompu - koppu = kompu pōṉṟa marakkiḷai, oru kātaṇi
kompu - kampu = kōl, taṭi (pāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakku).
kampu - kampam = tūṇāka nāṭṭum perumarat taṭi.
kompu - kompaṉ = āṇpiḷḷai, camarttaṉ.
koḷ - koṭu - koṭumai = vaḷaivu, neṟitiṟampal, aṭṭūḻiyam.
koṭuvāḷ, koṭukkāyppuḷi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ parupporuṭ kōṇalaiyum : koṭuṅkōl, koṭumpāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟil nuṇporuṭ kōṇalaiyum; koṭumaic col kuṟittal kāṇka.
koṭu - koṭukku = 1. vaḷainta koṭṭum uṟuppu; 2. aḻakiya āṭaittoṅkal. kaccai puṉaintatilē viṭṭāṉ peruṅ koṭukku (tiruvālavā. 30:9). 3. toṅkum kiḻical.
koṭukkaṉ = tēḷ, koṭukki = tēṭkoṭukki eṉṉum ceṭi. koṭukku - o.nō: E.crook, ME, croc, ON. krokr, hook.
koṭu - koṭuku. koṭukutal = koṭumaiyātal. koṭuku veñcilai vaṭuka vēṭuvar. (tēvā. 918:1).
koṭu - koṭumpu = koṭumai
koṭu - koṭi = vaḷaintu nīṇṭu vaḷarum taṇṭu.
koṭu - koṭiṟu = vaḷainta paṟṟuk kuṟaṭu. paṟṟuk kuṟaṭu pōṉṟa vāyalaku (katuppu).
koḷ - koḷku - koṭku. koṭkutal = 1. cuḻalutal. vaḷiva laṅkoṭku mātiram (maṇi. 12:91). 2. cūḻa varutal. kāluṇavā kac cuṭaroṭu koṭkum (puṟam. 43:3). 3. cuṟṟit tirital. koṭum puli koṭkum vaḻi (ciṟupañ. 80). o.nō: veḷ veḷku - veṭku.
koḷḷutal = 1. vaḷaital, 2. cuḻalutal, 3. tirital.
koḷ - koṭpu = 1. cuḻaṟci. koṭpuṟu kaliṉap pāymā (kamparā. mitilai. 13). 3. cuṟṟittirikai. (tivā.).
koḷ - koṭku - kokku = vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa nīrppaṟavai.
koṭku - koṭki - kokki = vaḷainta koḷuvi.
o.nō : kokki - E. hook, OE. hoc, MLG. hok, Du, hock.
koḷ - koṭṭai. o.nō : vaḷ - vaṭṭai
koṭṭai = 1. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa vitai. 2. av vitai pōṉṟa uṟuppu. 3. uruṇṭa talaiyaṇai. pañciṉ neṭṭaṇaiyarukāk koṭṭaikaḷ parappi (patiṉo. tiruviṭaima. mum. 19). 4. āṭait tumpiṉ maṇi muṭiccu. koṭṭaik karaiya paṭṭuṭai nalki (poruna. 155.). 5. pātak kuṟaṭṭiṉ kumiḻ. pavaḻak koṭṭaip poṟcerup pēṟṟi (peruṅ. makata. 22 : 202). 6. pañcuc curuḷ. `koṭṭait talaippāl koṭuttu (tiv. periyāḻ. 3:5:1). 7. uruṇṭa paruvaṭivam. koṭṭaiyeḻuttu; (u.va.).
koḷ - kōḷ = 1. cuṟṟivarum allatu uruṇṭaiyāṉa viṇcuṭar allatu vēṟulakam. ellāk kōḷum nalvaḻi nōkka (peruṅ ilāvāṇa. 11:70). 2. tiṅkaḷaic cūḻum kōṭṭai. matiyaṅ kōḷvāy vicumpiṭai (cīvaka. 1098).
kōḷ - kōḷam = 1. uruṇṭai. 2. uyirmeykaḷil (pirāṇikaḷil) nīr curakkum tacaippakuti.
kōḷam - va. kōla (gola).
kōḷam - kōḷakam = 1. uruṇṭa miḷaku. 2. maṇṭala viriyaṉ. (piṅ.)
kōḷam - va. kōlaka (miḷaku), kōlaka(pa) (maṇṭala viriyaṉ)
kōḷakam - kōḷakai = 1. vaṭṭa vaṭivam. aṇṭa kōḷakaip puṟattatāy (kamparā. akalikaip 60). 2. kimpurip pūṇ vaḷaiyam. 3. maṇṭalip pāmpu. (cūṭā.).
kōḷakai - va. kōlaka (g).
kōḷ - kōḷā = 1. naṟumaṇap paṇṭaṅ kalantatum iṟaicci yuḷḷiṭṭatumāṉa uṇṭaiyuṇavu. 2. kañcāvuruṇṭai.
kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṇutal = 1. cāytal. 2. vaḷaital. 3. neṟi tavaṟutal. 4. māṟupaṭutal. 5. veṟuttal. kōṇik kōṇik kōṭi koṭuppatilum kōṇāmaṟ kāṇi koṭuppatu mēl. (paḻa).
kōṇ = 1. vaḷaivu. kōṇārpiṟai (tiruvāca 16:5). 2. koṭuṅ kōṉmai. (tivā.). 3. kōṇam mukkō ṇivartaru vaṭṭam (kuṟṟā: tala. parācat. 3). 4. kūṉ.
kōṇaṉ = kūṉaṉ (piṅ.).
kōṇ - o.nō: Gk. gonia, angle.
kōṇ - kōṇam = 1. mūlai. kōṇa mottilaṅkōr muḻatti ṉiṉ (tiruvālavā. 15:2). 2. otukkuppuṟamāṉa iṭam. kōṇam - va. kōṇa.
kōṇam - kāṇam = 1. vaḷainta koḷḷukkāy. 2. koṭpayaṟu (pāṇṭivaḻakku).
kōṇ - kōṇai = 1. vaḷaivu. 2. kōṇal. 3. koṭumai. (aka.ni.). 4. tollai. kōṇai perituṭaittem perumāṉaik kūṟutalē (tiv. tiruvāy. 2:5:10).
kōṇai - kōṇaiyaṉ = māṟupaṭṭa kuṇañ ceyal karut tuṭaiyavaṉ.
kōṇ - kōṭu. o.nō: pāṇ - pāṭu.
kōṭutal = 1. kōṇutal, 2. vaḷaital, koṭum puruvaṅ kōṭāmaṟaippiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 1086) 2. neṟi allatu naṭunilai tavaṟutal. kōṭāruṅkōṭik karuviṉaiya rākiyārc cārntu (nālaṭi. 124). kōṭāmai cāṉṟōrk kaṇi (kuṟaḷ. 118). 3. cūḻtal. 4. vaṭṭamātal.
kōṭu = 1. vaḷaivu. 2. vaḷainta caṅku. kōṭu muḻaṅ kimiḻicai yeṭuppum (patiṟṟup. 50:25). 3. piṟaimati. kōṭumilaintāṉ (tirukkō. 149). 4. vilaṅkiṉ kompu. kōṭṭiṭaiyāṭiṉai kūttu (tiv. iyaṟ. tiruvirut. 21). 5. yāṉai maruppu (tantam). matta yāṉaiyiṉ kōṭum (tēvā. 39 : 1) 6. marakkiḷai. (piṅ.). 7. yāḻt taṇṭu. makara yāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi (maṇi 4:56). 8. eḻuttiṉ kompuk kuṟiyīṭu. vilaṅku peṟṟum kōṭu peṟṟum puḷḷi peṟṟum (tol. eḻut. 17, urai). 9. mayirc curuḷmuṭi kuraṟ kūntaṟ kōṭu (kalit. 72:20). 10. vaḷainta kuḷakkarai. kōṭiṉṟi nīr niṟaintaṟṟu (kuṟaḷ. 523). 11. kuḷam. kōṭelā niṟaiyak kuvaḷai malarum (tēvā. 425:4). 12. āṭaikkarai, koṭun tāṉaik kōṭṭaḻakum (nālaṭi. 131). 13. varampu. (piṅ.). 14. vari. 15. vaṭṭāṭum kaṭṭam. kōṭiṉṟi vaṭṭāṭal koḷva tokkum (tāyu. niṉai. 2). 16. kālavaṭṭam. kaliyukak kōṭṭunāḷ (T.A.S.H.P. 5). 17. naṭunilai tiṟampukai. kōṭi ṟīk kūṟṟam (nālaṭi. 5). 18. koṭumai. kōṭaṟa vaitta kōṭāk koḷkaiyum (patiṟ. 37:11). 19. varampu pōṉṟa pakkam. kōṭuya raṭuppu (puṟam. 164).
kōṭu - kōṭṭam = 1. vaḷaivu. marattiṉ kaṉakkōṭṭat tīrkku nūl (naṉ.25), mitivaṇṭic cakkaram kōṭṭampaṭṭirukkiṟatu. (ulaka vaḻakku). 2. vaṇakkam (uṭalvaḷaivu). muṉṉōṉ kaḻaṟkē. kōṭṭan tarunaṅkuṭimuṭi veṟpaṉ (tirukkōvai. 156. 3. vaṇaruṟuppuḷḷa allatu vaḷainta taṇṭuḷḷa yāḻ. (piṅ.). 4. vaḷainta allatu cūḻnta kuḷakkarai allatu ērikkarai. uyar kōṭṭattu..... vāṉ poykai (paṭṭiṉap. 36). 5. maṉak kōṇal. uṭkōṭṭa miṉmai peṟiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 119(. 6. naṭunilai tiṟampukai. kōviṉuk karuṅkalaṅ kōṭṭa millatu. (tēvā. 1182:2). 7. vēli cūḻnta tōṭṭam. (piṅ.). 8. araṇvēli cūḻnta pācaṟai. (piṅ.). 9. matil cūḻnta paḷḷipaṭai allatu kallaṟai. cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunilaik kōṭṭamum (maṇi. 6:59). 10. matil cūḻnta kōyil. kōḻic cēvaṟ koṭiyōṉ kōṭ ṭamum (cilap. 14:10). 11. matil cūḻnta ciṟaiccālai. kōṉṟamar nikaḷa mūḻkik kōṭṭattuk kuraṅku (cīvaka. 262). 12. cālai. aruñciṟaik kōṭṭat takavayiṟ pukuntu (maṇi. 19:133), aṟañ ceykōṭṭam (maṇi. patikam. 72). 13. matil cūḻnta nakar. (piṅ.). 14. vaṭṭāram allatu maṇṭalam eṉṉum nāṭu. (piṅ.). 15. matil cūḻnta iṭam. 16. iṭam. (piṅ.).
iṅṅaṉam, kōyilaik kuṟikkum kōṭṭam eṉṉum col, ul eṉṉum mūla vēriṉiṉṟum kul eṉṉum aṭivēriṉiṉ ṟum tōṉṟiya tūya teṉcollāyiruppiṉum, ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, atai āriya marapuppaṭi kōṣṭṭa (koshta) eṉṟu tirittu vaṭa collākak kāṭṭi yuḷḷatu.

kōṭṭi
koḷuttu - koḷutti = koḷuttukai. i. toḻiṟpeyar
īṟu o.nō: pōṟṟu - pōṟṟi = pōṟṟukai.
koḷutti - kōṭṭi (marūu).
o.nō: puḻaikkai - pūṭkai.
kōṭṭi - aṟivuṟuttal, coṟpoḻivu, avaippēccu.
vaṟuṅkōṭṭi (nālaṭi. 155)
pēccu - vīrakkōṭṭi pēcuvār (kampa. uyut. māyā. 13)
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kōṭṭi eṉṉuñ collaik kōṣṭi (gosth) eṉṟu tirittu kōṣamiṭum (āravārikkum) kūṭṭam eṉṟu poruḷum poruṭkaraṇiyamum kūṟuvar. tamiḻil kōṭṭi koḷal eṉpatu aṟivu koḷuttutalai mēṟ koḷḷutal allatu avaikkaṇ uraiyāṟṟutalaik kuṟikku mēyaṉṟi āravārippuk kūṭṭattaik koḷḷutal eṉṟu poruḷ paṭātu. aṅṅaṉam vaṭavar kūṟum poruḷaiyē koḷḷiṉum, atu teṉ collākumēyaṉṟi vaṭacol ākātu.
koḷḷutal = ka. ottal. vaṇṭiṉam yāḻ koṇṭa koṉaḷ (paripā. 11 : 125)
2. poruntutal koḷḷāta koḷḷā tulaku (kuṟaḷ. 470)

kōṭṭi ka. oruvarōṭu kūṭiyirukkai. taṉ tuṇaivi kōṭṭi liṉīṅki.) (cīvaka. 1025) 2. kūṭṭam (piṅ.). (ti.ma. 238)

kōṭṭai 
kōṭṭai - kōṭṭa
kōṭutal = viḷaital. kōṭu - kōṭṭai = 1. nilāvaic cūḻnta vaṭṭakkōṭu.
cantiraṉōr kōṭṭai. kaṭṭi (koṇṭal viṭu. 72). 2. vaṭṭa māṉa neṟkūṭu.
ulavākkōṭṭai (tiruvālavā. 50:13). 3. neṟkutir. 4. oru mukattalaḷavai.
itu mutalil oru neṟkutiraḷavāyiruntu, iṉṟu 21 marakkāl koṇṭa aḷavaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. 5. araṇmaṉaiyai allatu nakaraic cūḻnta matilaraṇ. k., f., te. kōṭṭa. mā.vi. akaramutali kōṭṭa eṉṉuñ coṟku, vaḷaivu, kuṭicai, matilaraṇ eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, kuṭ (vaḷai) eṉpatai mūlamākak kāṭṭum. kōṭu eṉṉuñcol vaṭamoḻiyiliṉmaiyāl iṅṅaṉaṅ kāṭṭa nērntatu. kuṭ eṉpatu kuṭa eṉṉum teṉcoṟṟiripu. (va.va: 133).

te. kōṭi, marā. kōṭī, i. kuṭōṭ, ā. krōr (crore)

kōpanilaikaḷ 
kōpam ciṟitu poḻutu niṟpatu; ciṉam nīṭittu niṟkum kōpam; cīṟṟam cīṟiyeḻuṅ kōpam; vekuḷi allatu kāyvu allatu uruttiram nerupput taṉmaiyuḷḷa kaṭuṅkōpam; kotippu kaṇ pōṉṟa uṟaviṉarkkuc ceyyappaṭṭa koṭumaipaṟṟip poṅki yeḻuṅ kōpam; ericcal maṉattai uṟuttuṅ kōpam; kaṭuppu poṟāmaiyōṭu kūṭiya kōpam; kaṟam allatu vaṉmam paḻivāṅkuṅ kōpam; kaṟuvu taṇiyāk kōpam; kaṟuppu karuppaṉ mukam kaṟuttut tōṉṟum kōpam; cikappu allatu ceyir; civappiṉ mukam civantu tōṉṟuṅkōpam;
viḷam naccut taṉmaiyāṉa kōpam; veṟi aṟiviḻanta kōpam; muṉivu allatu muṉaivu peṟuppōṭu kūṭiya kōpam; katam eṉṟum iyalpāṉa kōpam; kaṉivu mukañcuḷikkum kōpam; ceṟṟam allatu ceṟal pakaivaṉai aḻikkum kōpam; ūṭal maṉaivi kaṇava ṉoṭu kōpittuk koṇṭu uraiyāṭāta meṉkōpam; pulavi ūṭaliṉ vaḷarnta nilai; tuṉi ūṭaliṉ mutirnta nilai; caṭaivu uṟaviṉar kuṟaikūṟum amaitiyāṉa kōpam. (col: 53)
kōpuram 
 kōpuram = kōpura (g)
puraiyuyar pākum  (tol. (785).
puram = uyaramāṉa kaṭṭiṭam, mēṉmāṭam. kō = aracaṉ, talaimai.
kōpuram = aracaṉirukkum mēṉmāṭam, talaimaiyāṉa kāvaṟkottaḷam, atupōṉṟa kōyiṟ kōpuram.
mā.vi. akaramutali, kōpura eṉṉum collai āvaikkuṟikkum kō eṉṉum colliṉkīḻk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. mutaṉmutaṟ kōpuram kaṭṭappeṟṟatu teṉṉāṭṭilēyē. (va.va: 135)
kōyil 
kōvil allatu kōyil eṉpatu mutalāvatu aracaṉatu araṇ maṉaiyaiyē kuṟittatu. kō - aracaṉ. il - vīṭu. araca vaṇakkat tiṉpiṉ teyva vaṇakkam tōṉṟiyataṉāl aracamaṉaip peyar teyvamaṉaip peyarāyiṟṟu. (col. 24)
kōlam 
kōlam - kōla (g) = uruṇṭai.

kōlakam 
kōlakam - kōlaka (g) = uruṇṭai.
kōḷakam = uruṇṭa miḷaku, maṇṭala viriyaṉ.
kōḷakai = 1. vaṭṭa vaṭivam.
 aṇṭakōḷakaip puṟattatāy (kamparā. akalikaip. 60). 2. yāṉaikkimpuri. 3. maṇṭalippāmpu. (va.va: 135)
kōli viḷaiyāṭṭu
pāṇṭināṭṭu muṟai
āṭṭiṉ peyar : kallāluṅ kaṇṇāṭiyālum iyaṉṟa ciṟṟuruṇṭai kaḷait teṟittum uruṭṭiyum āṭum āṭṭu, kōli eṉappaṭum. (kōli = uruṇṭai).
āṭuvār tokai : ciṟuvaruḷḷum iḷaiñaruḷḷum, perum pāṉmai iruvarum ciṟupāṉmai mēṟpaṭṭavarum itai āṭuvar.
āṭukaruvi : oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu ēṟattāḻa nālaṭit tolaivil, akalaḷavāṉa vāyum ōraṅkula āḻamumuḷḷaṉavāka, varicaiyāy nilattiṟ killappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kuḻikaḷum, āṭakaṉ ovvoru vaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
kōliyāṭṭiṉ ēṉai muṟaikaṭkuriya kuḻiyum, iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa aḷaviṉatē.
āṭiṭam : akaṉṟa muṟṟamum puṟakkaṭaiyum poṭṭalum itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : āṭakar, kuḻivaricaikku nērāṉa iru ticaikaḷuḷ oṉṟil, kaṭaicikkuḻikku mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku kacattolai viṟ kīṟappaṭṭa kōṭṭil niṉṟu koṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉtaṉ kōliyai akkuḻi nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttiyavar muṉṉum, vīḻttātavar piṉṉum, āṭal vēṇṭum. oru varum kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttāviṭiṉ, kuḻikku neruṅka uruṭṭiyavar muṉṉum, ataṟkaṭutta aṇmaikku uruṭṭiyavar piṉṉum āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭuvār palarāyiṉ, iṅṅaṉamē aṇmai cēymai muṟaippaṭi muṉṉum piṉṉum āṭal vēṇṭum.
mutalil āṭuvāṉ kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttātavaṉāyiṉ, taṉ kōliyaik kuḻikkuḷ teṟittu vīḻttiyapiṉ, ataṟkaṭutta naṭukkuḻikkuḷḷum, ataṉpiṉ ataṟkaṭutta etirppakka iṟutik kuḻikkuḷḷum, piṉpu toṭarntu muṉṉum piṉṉumāka aṭuttaṭutta kuḻikkuḷḷum, pattām eṇvarai muṉpōṉṟē vīḻttavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vīḻttuvataṟku nāṉkumuṟai muṉṉum piṉṉumākat ticai tirumpa nērum. pattām vīḻttu naṭukkuḻikkuḷ nikaḻum. ataṉpiṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyait taṉ kōliyāl teṟittu aṭittuviṭiṉ kelippākum. pala etiri kaḷāyiṉ av aṉaivar kōliyaiyum aṭittal vēṇṭum.
mutalil āṭuvāṉ ētēṉum oru kuḻikkuḷ vīḻttat tavaṟiṉ, etiri āṭal vēṇṭum. etiriyum, tavaṟiṉ mutalāvāṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam tavaṟuntoṟum āṭakaṉ māṟuvāṉ.
oruvaṉ āṭumpōtu etiriyiṉ kōli arukiliruppiṉ, atu aṭuttamuṟai kuḻikkuḷ vīḻvatait taṭukkumāṟum, ataṉ aṭiyiṉiṉṟu tappumāṟum, ataṉai aṭittut tolaiviṟ pōkkiviṭuvatu vaḻakkam. āṭṭiṉ iṭaiyilāyiṉum iṟutiyi lāyiṉum etiriyiṉ kōliyai aṭikkat tavaṟiṉ, etiri āṭal vēṇṭum. etiriyiṉ kōli tolaivilirukkumpōtu atai aṭikkum āṟṟal allatu uṟutiyillāviṭiṉ, taṉ kōliyaic caṟṟē muṉtaḷḷi aṭuttamuṟai etiriyiṉ aṭikkut tappumāṟu ceyvatumuṇṭu.
oruvaṉ tavaṟi maṟṟoruvaṉ āṭumpōtu, āṭṭiṉ toṭakkat til āṭiya muṟaippaṭiyē āṭal vēṇṭum.
āṭuvār iruvarāyiṉum palarāyiṉum tōṟpavaṉ oruvaṉē. palarāyiṉ, iṟutiyil tōṟpavaṉoḻinta ēṉaiyarellārum kelikkumvarai āṭṭut toṭarum.
āṭṭu muṭintapiṉ, tōṟṟavaṉ kelittavariṭam muṭṭuvāṅkal vēṇṭum. tōṟṟavaṉ taṉ muṭṭik kaiyai, iru kaṇuviṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa pakuti kelittavarkku etirākat tōṉṟumāṟu, nilattil ūṉṟi vaittukkoṇṭirukka, kelittavar tam kōliyāl avaṉ muṭṭiyil aṭippar. itu muṭṭuppōṭutal eṉappaṭum. potuvāka mūṉṟu muṭṭu aṭippatu vaḻakkam. muṭṭup pōṭuvatuṭaṉ ōr āṭṭai muṭiyum. aṭutta āṭṭai āṭuvatu āṭakariṉ viruppattaip poṟuttatu. oru viḷai yāṭṭai oru muṟai āṭi muṭippatu ōr āṭṭai eṉappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : orukāl, arukiluḷḷa paḷḷattilirukkum kāṭṭuppaṟavaiyaik kaiyil villillāviṭattu viralkoṇṭu kallāl teṟikkum vēṭṭai viṉaiyiṉiṉṟu, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : viral narampu uraṅkoḷvatum, kuṟi tappāmal teṟittaṭikkap payilvatum, iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
cōḻa koṅku nāṭṭu muṟai
cōḻa koṅku nāṭukaḷiṟ potuvākak kōliyaik kuṇṭu allatu kōlikkuṇṭu eṉṟum, kōliyāṭṭattaik kuṇ ṭāṭṭam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. av āṭṭam, pēntā, añcala kuñcam, irukuḻiyāṭṭam, mukkuḻiyāṭṭam mutaliya pala vakaippaṭum.
	I. pēntā
 (i) caturap pēntā
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēntā eṉṉum nīḷcaturak kōṭṭiṟkuḷ uruṭṭiyāṭuṅ kōliyāṭṭu pēntā eṉappaṭum. itai vaṭṭap pēntāvuṭaṉ oppu nōkkic caturap pēntā eṉpar. itu koṅku nāṭṭil ciṟappāy viḷaiyāṭappaṭuvatu paṟṟi, tirucci vaṭṭārattil oru cilar itai īrōṭṭup pēntā eṉpatu muṇṭu.
āṭuvār tokai : ciṟāruḷḷum iḷaiñaruḷḷum iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭu karuvi : ēṟattāḻa ōraṭi nīḷamum mukkālaṭi akalamum nīṭṭup pōkkil naṭukkōṭum uḷḷa oru nīḷ caturakkōṭum, āṭuvāṉ ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām. peruṅkōliyum kallumāyiṉ, teṟittaṭikkap paṭāmal uruṭṭiyaṭikkappaṭum. pēntāviṟku ēṟattāḻap pattaṭittolaivil ōraṭi nīḷam oru kuṟuṅkōṭu kīṟappeṟum. atu utti yeṉappaṭum.
āṭiṭam : āṭiṭam pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk kōliyāṭṭiṟkup pōṉṟē itaṟkum.
āṭu muṟai : āṭakar iruvarum, uttiyeṉṉum kōṭṭil mēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyk kōliyaip pēntā nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. pēntāviṟku uḷḷē allatu arukē yār kōli niṉṟatō, avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭu āṭal vēṇṭum. pēntāviṟku veḷiyē allatu mika veḷiyē yār kōli niṉṟatō, avaṉ taṉ kōliyaip pēntāviṉ naṭuk kōṭṭu naṭuvil vaittal vēṇṭum.
āṭum valiyuṭaiyavaṉ mīṇṭum uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu taṉ kōliyaip pēntā nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. kōli pēntāviṟkuḷḷēṉum pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭaikkuḷ ḷēṉum pōy niṟpiṉ tavaṟām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟip pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭaik kappāṟpōy niṟpiṉ, pēntāviṟ kuḷḷirukkuṅ kōliyai aṭittal vēṇṭum. avvāṟu aṭitta piṉ, aṭikkappaṭṭa kōliyaṉṟi aṭitta kōli pēntāviṟkuḷḷum pēntāvi ṉiṉṟu nālviraṟ kiṭaikkuḷḷum niṟṟal kūṭātu; niṟpiṉ tavaṟām. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa maṉṟip pēntāviṉiṉṟu nālviraṟkiṭaik kappāṟ pōy niṟpiṉ, aṭittavaṉ mīṇṭum uttikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉmēl niṉṟukoṇṭu, etiriyiṉ kōliyai aṭittal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam aṭikkumuṉ, etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟku veḷi yil niṟpiṉ, pēntā cettu āṟu mācam eṉṟu kūṟutal vēṇ ṭum; allākkāl tavaṟām. tavaṟiya pōtellām āṭakaṉ māṟal vēṇṭum.
aṭippavaṉ mūṉṟām muṟai uttiyil niṉṟu aṭikkumpōtu, etiriyiṉ kōliyai mūṉṟu taram toṭarntu aṭittal vēṇ ṭum. ataṉāl etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟkut tolaiviṟ pōkkappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
mūṉṟām muṟai uttiyil niṉṟu aṭikkumpōtu, etiriyiṉ kōli pēntāviṟkuḷ illākkāl, aṭikkuṅ kōli pēntā viṟkuṭ ceṉṟu niṟpiṉum kuṟṟamillai.
oruvaṉ pēntāviṉ arukiṟpōy niṉṟa taṉ kōliyāl etiriyiṉ kōliyai aṭikkumpōtu, pēntāmēṟ kaipaṭum eṉṟu aṭippavaṉ muntic colliṉum, pēntāmēṟ kaipaṭak kūṭātu eṉṟu aṭikkappaṭum kōlikkāraṉ muntic colli ṉum, avvāṟē niṟaivēṟṟappaṭal vēṇṭum.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli oru paḷḷattiliruppiṉ, aṭippavaṉ nō eṉṟu colvāṉ. uṭaṉē, aṭikkappaṭum kōliyai etiri eṭuttu aṭippavaṉukku etirākap paḷḷanērukkuc camanilat til vaittal vēṇṭum.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli ōr iṭukkiliruppiṉ aṭippavaṉ cāṇcūṭi caṭāpuṭā eṉṟu colvāṉ. uṭaṉē etiri atai eṭuttu aṭippavaṉukkut teriyum paṭi vaittal vēṇṭum. cāṇcūṭi caṭāpuṭā illai eṉṟu etiri muntic colli ṭiṉ, atai eṭuttu vaikka vēṇṭiyatillai.
oruvaṉ taṉ kōliyāl aṭikkumpōtu, atai vaḻic celvōr yārēṉum taṭuttuviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ taṭuttāl tūr eṉṟu colvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, avvaḻic celvōr taṉ kālāl atait taḷḷumpaṭi vēṇṭappaṭuvar.
aṭikkappaṭum kōli uttikkuk kiṭṭē irukkum pōtu ataṟkuriyavaṉ avviṭattiṉiṉṟu uttikkut tāṇṭiviṭiṉ, aṭippavaṉ taṉ kōliyait taṉ kaṇṇil vaittāvatu uyarttiya kaiyil vaittāvatu etiriyiṉ kōliyiṉ mēliṭ ṭaṭittal vēṇṭum. av aṭi tavaṟiṉ, mīṇṭum nilamaṭṭattiṟ kaiyāl teṟittaṭikkalām. av vaṭiyum tavaṟiṉ tōlviyām.
mūṉṟāmmuṟai uttiyil niṉṟu aṭippavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyait toṭarntu mūṉṟu taram aṭittu viṭiṉ etiri taṉ kōliyait taṉ muṭṭikkaiyāl oru taṭavai allatu pala taṭavai taḷḷip pēntāviṟkuḷ koṇṭu vantu niṟuttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam niṟuttiviṭiṉ, avaṉē kelittavaṉāvaṉ; allākkāl tōṟṟavaṉāvaṉ. muṭṭikkaiyāl kōliyait taḷḷuvatai muṭṭitaḷḷal eṉpar.
 (ii) vaṭṭappēntā
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pēntā eṉappaṭum vaṭṭakkōṭṭiṟkuḷ uruṭṭiyāṭuṅ kōliyāṭṭu vakai vaṭṭappēntāvām. itu caturap pēntāvuṭaṉ oppunōkki vaṭṭap pēntā eṉappaṭ ṭatu.
āṭuvār tokai : mūvar mutal ettuṇaip pērāyiṉum itai āṭalām. āyiṉum, aivarkkuk kuṟaiyātum patiṉmarkkuk kūṭātum iruppiṉ ciṟappām.
āṭukaruvi : āṭakaruḷ ovvoruvaṉukkum pala kōlikaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟōṭu tel eṉṉum oru peruṅ kōli ovvoru vaṉiṭattumāvatu ellārkkum potuvāka vāvatu iruttal vēṇṭum.
ēṟattāḻa mukkaca viṭṭamuḷḷa oru vaṭṭakkōṭu kīṟappaṭum. ataṉ naṭuvil oru kuḻi killappaṭum. vaṭṭattiṉ aṭiyil kāl vaṭṭam amaiyumāṟu oru kuṟukkuk kōṭum kīṟappaṭum.
kāl vaṭṭamuḷḷa pakkattil, vaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu ēṟattāḻa mukkacat tolaivil, utti kīṟappaṭum. ataṟku mēlum iru pakkattum kaviyumāṟu oru talaikīḻāṉa pakarak kōṭu iṭappaṭum. atu mūṭi eṉappaṭum. atai iṉṟu ṭāppu (Top) eṉṉum āṅkilac collāṟ kuṟippar.
āṭiṭam : muṟṟamum puṟakkaṭaiyum poṭṭalum veḷi nilamum itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : āṭakarellārum mutaṉ mutal uttiyil niṉṟu koṇṭu , ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyai vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuḻiyai nōkki uruṭṭal vēṇṭum. yār kōli kuḻikku aṇṇaṇittāy (atavātu mika neruṅki) niṟkiṉṟatō, avaṉ muṉṉum; ataṟku aṭuttu niṟkuṅ kōlikkāraṉ piṉṉum; ataṟkaṭuttu niṟkuṅ kōlikkāraṉ ataṉ piṉṉumāka; iṅṅaṉam kuḻiyaṇmai varicaippaṭi ellārum āṭal vēṇṭum. kōliyai uruṭṭumpōtu oruvaṉ kōli iṉ ṉoruvaṉatai aṭittuviṭṭāl, ellār kōliyum mīṇṭum ovvoṉṟāy uruṭṭappaṭum. kōlikaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu aṭipaṭuvatu caṭapuṭā eṉappaṭum.
āṭuvār varicai yoḻuṅku ivvāṟu tuṇiyappaṭṭa piṉ, muntiyāṭukiṟavaṉiṭam ēṉaiyōrellārum āḷukkoru kōli koṭuttu viṭal vēṇṭum. avaṉ avaṟṟuṭaṉ taṉ kōliyaiyuñ cērttu, ellāvaṟṟaiyum mottamāy vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ uruṭṭu vāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, tellāl (atāvatu kuṇṭāl) mukkāl vaṭṭattiluḷḷa kōlikaḷuḷ etaiyēṉum evaṟṟaiyēṉum aṭippāṉ. kāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷavaṟṟai aṭittal kūṭātu; aṭippiṉ, paccāvām. ataṟku oru kōli pōṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, aṭuttavaṉ āṭuvāṉ.
uruṭṭuvāṉ yārāyiṉum, mūṭiyai mitikkavāvatu tāṇṭavā vatu kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyiṉ tavaṟiyavaṉāvaṉ. ataṉāl aṭuttavaṉ āṭa nērum. mūṭi potuvāka mutaliṟ pōṭappaṭuva tillai. palamuṟai colliyuṅkēḷātu, oruvaṉ uttikku muṉṉāṟ kāl vaittu muṉpuṟamākac cāyntu aṭippiṉ, mūṭi pōṭappaṭum. atu pōṭappaṭṭa piṉ atai mitippiṉ allatu tāṇṭiṉ paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṟku oru kāy pōṭṭu viṭavēṇṭum.
uruṭṭappaṭṭa kōlikaḷuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟu nērē kuḻiyil viḻuntuviṭiṉ, veṟṟiyām. avaṉ kāykaḷai aṭikkat tēvai yillai. uruṭṭiṉavaṉ tirumpavum āṭalām.
mukkāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷa kōliyai allatu kōlikaḷai aṭippiṉ, aṭipaṭṭa kōliyaṉaittum vaṭṭattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyēṟiviṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam veḷiyēṟiviṭiṉ atu oru veṟṟiyām. kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭālum kāyaṭittālum, ēṉaiyōrellārum kelittavaṉukku ovvoru kāy koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻiyuḷ pōṭṭuk kāyum aṭittu viṭiṉ iraṭṭaik kelippātaliṉ, ēṉaiyōr ivvirukāy koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. mukkāl vaṭṭattuḷ uḷḷa kāy kaḷai aṭikkumpōtu, kuḻik kāyaiyum aṭikkalām; veḷik kāyaiyum aṭikkalām. veṉṟavaṉ eṉṟum tirumpiyāṭuvāṉ.
kuḻiyilum pōṭāmal mukkāl vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ uḷḷa kāyai yum aṭikkāmal pōṉavaṉ, taṉ āṭṭattai iḻantu viṭuvāṉ. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. aṉṟu oruvarum kāy pōṭuvatillai.
aṭitta kāyāvatu aṭikkappaṭṭa kāyāvatu vaṭṭattaiviṭṭu veḷiyē pōkāviṭiṉ, paccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṟku oru kōli aṭuttavaṉiṭam koṭuttuviṭṭu, āṭṭattai viṭṭu viṭal vēṇṭum. kuḻiyiṟ pōṭṭaviṭattum aṭitta kāy allatu aṭikkappaṭṭa kāy veḷiyēṟāviṭiṉ, iraṭṭaip paccāvām. aṉṟu, aṭittavaṉ iru kāy koṭuttuviṭṭu āṭṭattai viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum. aṭuttavaṉ avaṟṟaiyuñ cērttu uruṭṭuvāṉ. avaṉ kelittuviṭiṉ, ap paccāk kāykaḷaiyum vaḻakkappaṭi koṭukkiṟavaṟṟaiyum eṭuttuk koḷḷalām; kelikkāviṭiṉ paccāk kāykaḷ paḻaṅkāy kaḷuṭaṉ cērttu uruṭṭappeṟum. evaṉ eṅṅaṉam kelip piṉum, paḻaṅkāykaḷai orupōtum kelippuk kāyākap peṟal muṭiyātu. āṭṭam muṭiyum varai, avai oru mutalpōl iruntukoṇṭēyirukkum. āṭṭam muṭintapiṉ, avaṉavaṉ mutaṟkāy avaṉavaṉaic cērum.
oruvaṉ kelittaviṭattu iṉṉoruvaṉukkup pōṭak kāyillā viṭṭāl, avaṉ kaṭaṉākavāvatu vilaikkāvatu piṟaṉiṭam vāṅkikkoḷḷalām; allākkāl, avaṉ mutalil iṭṭa kāyai iḻantuviṭuvāṉ.
ellārum āṭi muṭintapiṉ, oruvariṭamāvatu palariṭamāvatu kāy mikuti yāyc cērntirukkum. avaṟṟuḷ avaravar contak kāykku mēṟpaṭṭavellām kelippuk kāyākum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ: kōliyāṭṭiṉ potuppayaṉāka muṟkūṟap paṭṭavaṟṟoṭu, peruntokaiyāṉa kōlikaḷai eḷitāy īṭṭikkoḷvatum, ciṟu mutalīyiṭṭup pērūtiyam peṟum karuttuṟavum, iv āṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
 II. añcala kuñcam
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aintām eṇṇaic collum pōtu añcala kuñcam eṉṟu collappaṭum kōliyāṭṭu vakai, at toṭar moḻiyaiyē peyarākak koṇṭatu. itaṟku orē kuḻiyuḷḷamaiyāl, itu oṟṟaik kuḻiyāṭṭam eṉavumpaṭum.
āṭuvār tokai : ciṟuvaruḷḷum iḷaiñaruḷḷum iruvar itai āṭuvar.
āṭiṭam : pēntāviṟkuriyatē itaṟkum.
āṭukaruvi : pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk kōliyāṭṭiṟkuk kūṟiya aḷavuḷḷa oṟṟaik kuḻiyum, ovvoruvaṉukkum ovvoru kōliyum, itaṟkuriya karuvikaḷām.
āṭumuṟai : āṭakar, kuḻikku 8 aṭi allatu 10 aṭit tolaiviluḷḷa uttik kōṭṭiṉmēl niṉṟu koṇṭu, ovvoruvaṉāyt taṉ taṉ kōliyai uruṭṭal vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉatu kōli mutalilēyē kuḻikkuḷ viḻuntu viṭṭāl oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇām. ataṉ piṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyai aṭittu viṭṭāl pattām. atu paḻamākum. atōṭu ōr āṭṭai muṭiyum.
mutaliṟ kuḻikkuḷ viḻāviṭṭāl, kuḻikkuk kiṭṭa irukkiṟa kōlikkāraṉ muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. avaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyai aṭikkalām; allatu kuḻikkuṭ pōṭalām. etiri yiṉ kōliyait tavaṟātu aṭittuviṭiṉ aintām; aṅṅaṉa miṉṟik kuḻikkuṭ pōṭṭu viṭiṉ nāṉkām.
nāṉkām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉum aintām eṇṇi liruntu toṭaṅkiṉum, pattām eṇvarai toṭartal vēṇ ṭum. cila eṇkaṭkuk kuḻiyum cila eṇkaṭku aṭiyum ākum. kuḻi eṉpatu kuḻikku aṭittal. nāṉkām eṇṇi liruntu toṭaṅkiṉ, 5, 8, 9, 10 eṉpaṉa aṭiyām; 6, 7 eṉpaṉa kuḻiyām; aintām eṇṇiliruntu toṭaṅkiṉ 6, 7 eṉpaṉa kuḻiyām; 8, 9, 10 eṉpaṉa aṭiyām. pattāvatu, kōliyai aṭittaṟkup patilāka kuḻikkuḷ aṭippiṉ, mīṇṭum nāṉkām.
 kuḻikkuḷ aṭikkum pōtum kāyai (atāvatu etiriyiṉ kōli yai) aṭikkumpōtum tavaṟiviṭiṉ, aṭuttavaṉ āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭumpōtu, aintām eṇ mutal pattām eṇ varai ovvōr eṇṇiṟkum ovvoru maraput toṭarmoḻi kūṟikkoḷvatuṇṭu. avai,
 añcala kuñcam,
 aṟuvakka tāṭi,
 eḻuvakka maiṉā,
eṭṭakka kōṭṭai, (eṭṭōṭaṭṭak kōṭṭai)
tompaṉip pēṭṭai, (tomparap pēṭṭai, tompalap pāṭi).
tocukkaṭṭurācā, (tēciṅkurācā, campā viḻuntatu) eṉpaṉavām.
pattām aṭi tavaṟātu muntiyaṭittavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyai mūṉṟu taṭavai aṭittut taḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṭittut taḷḷap paṭṭa kōlikkāraṉ muṭṭi taḷḷa vēṇṭum; atāvatu taṉ kōliyait taṉ muṭṭik kaiyāl kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavai taḷḷik koṇṭu vantu kuḻikkuḷ vīḻtta vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam vīḻtti viṭiṉ kelippām; illāviṭiṉ tōṟpām.
pattām aṭi tavaṟātu aṭittavaṉ etiriyiṉ kōliyai aṭittut taḷḷumpōtu, etiri taṉ kōlineṭun tolaiviṟkup pōy viṭātavāṟu taṉ muṭṭikkaiyāl atait taṭukkalām; kālāl taṭuttal kūṭātu; kālāl taṭuppiṉ 7 taṭavai muṭṭi taḷḷal vēṇṭum.
muṭṭi taḷḷukiṟavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavai taḷḷiyum taṉ kōliyaik kuḻikkuṭ koṇṭu vantu niṟutta muṭiyāviṭiṉ, aṭittavaṉ atai mīṇṭum aṭittup pōkkuvāṉ. muṭṭi taḷḷiṉavaṉ mīṇṭum muṭṭi taḷḷuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam nāṉku muṟai aṭippum taḷḷum nikaḻalām. ovvoru muṟaikkum aṭippiṟkum taḷḷiṟkum kaṇakkuṇṭu.
mutaṉmuṟai 	3 aṭi 	3 taḷḷu
2 ām muṟai 	2 aṭi 	2 taḷḷu
3 ām muṟai 	1 aṭi 	1 taḷḷu
4 ām muṟai 	1 aṭi 	3 taḷḷu
muṭṭi taḷḷukiṟavaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa taṭavai taḷḷiyum taṉ kōliyaik kuḻikkuṭ koṇṭu vantu niṟutta muṭiyāviṭiṉ, kīḻvarumāṟu oru valakkārattaik kaiyāḷvatuṇṭu. avaṉatu kōli aṭikka muṭiyāta tolaivilirukkumpōtu, aṭikkiṟa vaṉ taṉakku vacatiyuṇṭākumāṟu mēluñ caṟṟut taḷḷac colvāṉ. appōtu taḷḷukiṟavaṉ, aṭikkiṟa vaṉukkut teriyātavāṟu taṉ kōliyai oru kaikkuḷiṭṭu maṟaittu iru kaikaḷaiyum muṭṭiyāka vaittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaic caṟṟu muṉṉāka eṭuttu vaittup pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ pōtātu eṉṟu colliṉ, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ iṉṉoru muṭṭik kaiyai muntiṉatiṉum caṟṟu muṉpāka eṭuttuvaittup pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ mīṇṭum pōtātu eṉṟu colliṉ, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ mīṇṭum piṉṉāka irukkum kaiyai muṉṉāka eṭuttu vaippāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ kōlik kai etuveṉṟum veṟuṅkai etuveṉṟum teriyāmal, pōtātu,; pōtātu eṉṟu mēlum mēlum collac collat, taḷḷukiṟavaṉ taṉ iru kaikaḷaiyum māṟi māṟi muṉṉāka eṭuttu vaittu, iṟutiyil kōlikkaiyaik kuḻimēl vaittukkoṇṭu, pōtumā? eṉṟu kēṭpāṉ. aṭikkiṟavaṉ kuḻik kaiyai veṟuṅkai eṉṟu karutikkoṇṭu, pōtum eṉpāṉ; uṭaṉē taḷḷukiṟavaṉ taṉ kōliyaik kuḻikkuḷ iṭṭuviṭṭut taṉ kaiyai eṭuttuviṭuvāṉ; aṭittavaṉ ēmāṟip pōvāṉ; taḷḷiṉavaṉ kelittu viṭuvāṉ. itaṟku mīṉ piṭittal eṉṟu peyar.
taḷḷukiṟavaṉ mīṉ piṭikkalāmā piṭikkak kūṭātā eṉpatu, muṭṭi taḷḷu muṉṉarē muṭivu ceyyappeṟum.
 III. irukuḻiyāṭṭam (iṣṭam)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cuvaraṭi araṅkiṟkuḷ iru kuḻi vaittāṭum kōliyāṭṭu vakai irukuḻiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum. itaṟku iṭṭam (iṣṭam) allatu kicēpi eṉṟum peyar.
iv āṭṭu perumpālum tirucci vaṭṭārattil āṭap peṟuvatākum.
āṭuvār tokai : ciṟāruḷḷum iḷaiñaruḷḷum iruvar allatu ( iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa) palar itai āṭuvar. ōr āṭṭaiyil iruvarē itai āṭa muṭiyumātalāl, palar āṭuvatāyiṉ ivviruvarākavē āṭak kūṭum.
āṭukaruvi : cuvaraṭi (allatu mēṭṭaṭi) nilattil, ēṟattāḻa 3 aṭi nīḷamum 2 aṭi akalamumuḷḷa oru nīḷ catura araṅkamaiyu māṟu ōr akaṉṟa pakarak kōṭu kīṟik koḷḷal vēṇṭum. av varaṅkiṉ neṭumpakkaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu cuvaraṭiyāyirukkum vaṇṇam, pakarattiṉ iru pakkak kōṭṭu muṉaikaḷum cuvarait toṭutal vēṇṭum. avvaraṅkiṟkuḷ iṭavalamāka iru kuḻikaḷ killal vēṇṭum.
araṅkiṟku ēṟattāḻa mukkacat tolaivil ōraṭi nīḷam utti kīṟappaṭum. ataṟku ṭāppu (Top) eṉṉum mūṭi iṭappaṭum.
oruvaṉ āṭumpōtu iṉṉoruvaṉukkuk kōli tēvai yāyirāmaiyiṉ, āṭakar ellārkkum potuvāka mukkōli yiruntāṟ pōtum. avaṟṟuḷ, iraṇṭu uruṭṭuvaṉa; oṉṟu avaṟṟai aṭippatu. aṭikkuṅkōli caṟṟup peritāka virukkum. ataṟkut tiruccip pāṅkaril kuṭṭu allatu tel eṉṟu peyar.
āṭiṭam: cuvaraṭi nilamum mēṭṭaṭi nilamum itai āṭumiṭamām.
āṭumuṟai : tōṟṟavaṉ kelittavaṉukku ivvaḷavu kācu koṭut tal vēṇṭumeṉṟu muṉṉatāka muṭivu ceytukoṇṭu, uṭaṉpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyō tiruvuḷac cīṭṭiṉpaṭiyō yārēṉum oruvaṉ muntiyāṭuvaṉ. āṭuvār ellārkkum kōli potuvā yiṉ, kōlikkuc contakkāraṉ muntiyāṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
āṭukiṟavaṉ uttiyil niṉṟukoṇṭu, mutalāvatu iru ciṟu kōlikaḷaiyum araṅkiṟkuḷ uruṭṭi, avaṟṟuḷ etai aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu etiriyaik kēṭpāṉ. av iraṇṭiṉuḷ āṭukiṟavaṉukkuk kiṭṭa iruppatai aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉ kīḻ eṉṟum, caṟṟu eṭṭa (atāvatu tolaivil) iruppatai aṭikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉ mēl eṉṟum, etiri colvāṉ. kīḻ eṉpatiṟkup patilāka etir eṉṟum colvatuṇṭu. āṭukiṟavaṉ etiri coṉṉa kōliyai aṭittu viṭṭāṟ kelittavaṉāvaṉ. avaṉukkup pēciṉapaṭi etiri kācaik koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum. mutalil eṟiyappaṭṭa iru kōlikaḷum iruviralukku mēl iṭaiyiṭṭiruppiṉ, etiri coṉṉatait tāṉ aṭittal vēṇṭum; iruviraṟku uṭpaṭ ṭiruppiṉ, etaiyum aṭikkalām. iṅṅaṉam viruppa māṉatai aṭikkuṅ kāraṇam paṟṟiyō, iv āṭṭiṟku iṭṭam allatu kicēpi eṉṟu peyar.
iṭaiyīṭu iruviraṟkuṭpaṭṭa nilaiyil etaiyum aṭikkum pōtu iraṇṭilum paṭṭuviṭṭāl, paccara eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. atōṭu, aṭitta kōliyāyiṉum aṭikkappaṭṭa kōli yāyiṉum kuḻikkuḷ vīḻntuviṭiṉ, iraṭṭaippaccā eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. kuṟṟamellām tōlvittāṉamē.
mutalil eṟinta iru kōlikaḷum araṅkiṟku uḷvīḻiṉum veḷivīḻiṉum, mūṉṟāṅ kōli nērē kuḻikkuḷ viḻuntuviṭiṉ, eṟintavaṉukku etiri pēciṉa tokaiyai iraṭṭippāyk kaṭṭiviṭil vēṇṭum.
mutalil eṟinta oru kōlikaḷuḷ oṉṟu araṅkiṟku veḷiyē niṟpiṉ, etiri atai eṭuttu araṅkuk kōṭṭiṉ mēl vaippāṉ; allatu muṭṭikkaiyāl araṅkiṟkuḷ taḷḷi viṭuvāṉ. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉataṟku mūṭṭutal eṉṟu peyar.
aṭikkuṅ kōli mutal vīḻvilēyē araṅkiṟkuḷ viḻuntu viṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi ataṟku veḷiyē vīḻiṉ, atu veḷimaṭṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamātaliṉ āṭiṉavaṉ tōṟṟa vaṉāvaṉ.
mūṉṟāṅ kōli (atāvatu aṭikkuṅ kōli) araṅkiṟkuḷ kāyai aṭikkātu kōṭṭiṉmēl niṟpiṉ, kōṭu eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. atai laiṉ (line) allatu lāk (lock) eṉṉum āṅkilac collāṟ kuṟippar.
uttiyil niṉṟu aṭittavaṉ ataiviṭṭu nīṅkum pōtu, mūṭiyai mitikkātu ōreṭṭup piṉvaittu iṭamākavāvatu valamāka vāvatu cuṟṟi muṉvaral vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi mūṭiyai mitittuviṭiṉ tavaṟiṉavaṉāvaṉ.
āṭukiṟavaṉ aṭitta kōliyum iṉṉoṉṟum araṅkiṟkuḷ oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭṭukkoṇṭu niṟpiṉ, avaṉ avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai iṉṉoṉṟu alukkātavāṟu eṭuttal vēṇṭum. alukkiviṭiṉ tōṟṟavaṉāvaṉ. aluṅkāmal eṭuttaṟkāka iraṇṭiṟkum iṭaiyil ciṟitu maṇṇait tūvuvatu vaḻakkam. āṭṭiṟ kelittavaṉ maṟu āṭṭaiyil muntiyāṭal vēṇṭum. āṭakar palarāyiṉ, tōṟṟavaṉ nīṅki vēṟoruvaṉ etiriyāvaṉ.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kuṟi tappāmal uruṭṭiyaṭikkap payilvatum, oṉṟaiyoṉṟu toṭṭuniṟkum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟaip piṟitu allatu piṟa alukkātavāṟu eṭukkap paḻakuvatum, ivvāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
IV. mukkuḻiyāṭṭam
(i) cēlam vaṭṭāra muṟai.
 cuvar
 kuḻikaḷ
 araṅku
 ṭāppu
 utti
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cuvaraṭiyaṅkiṟkuḷ irukuḻikkup patilāka mukkuḻi vaittāṭum kōliyāṭṭa vakaiyē mukkuḻiyāṭṭam.
āṭu muṟai : mēṟkūṟiya iru kuḻiyāṭṭamum iṅkuk kuṟitta mukkuḻiyāṭṭamum oṉṟē. āyiṉ, iṭa vēṟupāṭu kāraṇa mākap piṉvarumāṟu mūvakai vēṟṟumaiyuṇṭu.
		tirucci 	cēlam

kōvaṉ - kōṉ = 1. iṭaiyaṉ. 2. aracaṉ.
kōṉ - kōṉār = iṭaiyar. kōṉ - kō = aracaṉ.
kōvaṉ eṉpataik kōpa eṉṟu tirittum piṉpu ataik kō + pa eṉṟu pirittum, kōvaip pātukāppavaṉ eṉṟu vaṭavar poruḷ kūṟuvatu poruntātu. kōvaṉ eṉpatu āyaṉ (ā +aṉ) eṉpatai yottatē.
āṅkilattil (tiyūttāṉiyattil) kō (OS), kū (OE) kau (E) eṉṟu eṭuppoliyiṉṟiyē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. vēta āriyat til tāṉ go eṉṟu eṭuttum gous eṉṟu tirintum olittiruk kiṉṟatu. (va.va: 131 - 132.)
kōvaṉ
āyaṉ ākkaḷaik kāppatu pōla aracaṉ makkaḷaik kātta lāl, aracaṉukkuk kōvaṉ eṉṟu peyar. kōvaṉ eṉpatu muṟaiyē kōṉ, kō eṉat tiriyum. kō - pacu. kōkkaḷai mēyppatu paṟṟi āyaṉukkuk kōṉ, kōṉār eṉṉum orumaip peyarum uyarvup paṉmaip peyarum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. āyaṉaik kuṟikkum kōṉ eṉṉum collum aracaṉaik kuṟikkum kōṉ eṉṉum collum oṉṟē. aracaṉukkum kuṭikaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu āyaṉukkum āniraikkum uḷḷa toṭarpu pōṉṟatu eṉpatē, kōṉ eṉṉum collāl kuṟikkappaṭum karuttām. aracaṉ kaiyiluḷḷa kōlum āyaṉ kaikkōl pōṉṟatē. kōl eṉṉum col ākupeyarp poruḷil aracāṭciyaik kuṟikkum. nērmaiyāṉa āṭci ceṅkōl eṉṟum koṭumai yāṉa āṭci koṭuṅkōl eṉṟum collappaṭum.
(kiṟittavak kurumārai ik karuttup paṟṟiyē āṅkilattil Shepherd eṉṟum Pastor eṉṟum aḻaippar. kiṟittava capaiyār āṭukaḷum, accapaik kurumār mēypparum pōlvar. nāṉē nalla mēyppaṉ eṉṟār ēcu perumāṉār.)
iṉi, civaperumāṉum uyirkaḷai yellām kāppatil āyaṉai allatu aracaṉai oppavarātaliṉ kōvaṉ eṉappaṭuvar. uyirkaḷai allatu āṉmākkaḷaip potuppaṭap pacu eṉpatum, civaperumāṉaip pacupati eṉpatum vaṭamoḻi vaḻakku. (col. 4-5.)
ṅammutal
ulaka vaḻakkiluḷḷa ovvoru moḻiyilum coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum nāḷaṭaivil māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. am māṟṟam tamiḻil marūu, tiripu, citaivu, pōli eṉap palpeyarāṟ kūṟappaṭum. moḻikaḷiṉ vaṭivu māṟavē avaṟṟiṉ mutaliṭai kaṭai yeḻuttukaḷum māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu collāmē pōtarum.
piṟamoḻikaḷil moḻimutaliṭaikaṭai yeḻuttukaṭku varam pillai. tamiḻilō av varampuṇṭu. oru taṉimoḻiyiṉ allatu taṉittu vaḻaṅkak kūṭiya moḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu maṟṟōreḻut tāyt tiriyiṉ, attiripeḻuttē mutaleḻuttāyk koḷḷap paṭum. ilakkaṇam iṭantariṉ.
e-ṭu:		naṇṭu - ñaṇṭu.
araiñāṇ (araināṇ), paiññīli (painnīli), cēyñalūr (cēynallūr) mutaliya toṭarmoḻikaḷiluḷḷa varumoḻikaḷ ñāṇ, ñīli, ñallūr eṉat tam tirinta vaṭivilum taṉittu vaḻaṅkun takuti ciṟituṭaimaiyiṉ avaṟṟiṉ mutaliluḷḷa tiripeḻuttukaḷum moḻimutaleḻuttukaḷākak koḷḷap paṭal kūṭum. āṉāl, oru toṭarmoḻiyiluḷḷa varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu moḻi mutalallā eḻuttākat tiriyiṉ, av vitimutal iyalpumutalākak koḷḷappaṭātu. īṇṭu iyalpu mutal vitimutal eṉpavaṟṟai iyalpīṟu vitiyīṟu eṉpavaṟṟai yeṇṇi yuṇarka.
e-ṭu: 		al + tālam = aṟṟālam.
			viṇ + nāṭu = viṇṇāṭu.
ivaṟṟil ṟā, ṇā eṉṉum vitimutalkaḷ moḻi mutaleḻut tākāmai yuṇarka.
tolkāppiyar ciṟanta ārāycciyuṭaiya rātaliṉ, ṅakaram varu moḻiyiṉ vitimutalāy maṭṭum varuvatu kaṇṭu atai moḻi mutaleḻuttākak kūṟiyilar. naṉṉūlārō av vārāycci yiṉmaiyiṉ, vitimutalāy vanta ṅakarattai iyalpu mutaleṉa mayaṅkik kūṟiṉar.
ṅakaram aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam, eṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam eṉak cuṭṭu viṉāmutaṟ ṟoṭarmoḻikaḷil varumoḻi vitimutalā yirukkumēyaṉṟi, yāṇṭum taṉimoḻiyil iyalpu mutalāy varuvataṉṟu. aṅṅaṉam. iṅṅaṉam eṉpaṉa avvitam ivvitam eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvaṉavākum. avvaṇṇam. av vakai, avvitam, appaṭi eṉṉum toṭar moḻi kaḷiluḷḷa vaṇṇam. vakai, vitam, paṭi eṉṉum coṟkaḷ taṉittu vaḻaṅku vatu pōla, ṅaṉam eṉṉum col taṉittu vaḻaṅkuvataṉṟu. avaṉ ceyta vitam, avaṉ ceyta ṅaṉam eṉṉun toṭar kaḷaik kūṟik kāṇka.
aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliya toṭarmoḻikaḷil mellōcai ciṟantiruttaliṉ, melittal (Nasalization) ōcai mikutiyum payil kiṉṟa malaiyāḷa vaḻakkil orukāl ṅaṉam eṉṉum col taṉittu vaḻaṅkalāmeṉṟu karutip palaviṭattumuḷḷa malaiyāḷaraik kēṭṭatil avarkaḷum ṅaṉam eṉṉumcol tamiḻiṟpōlavē malaiyāḷattilum toṭarmoḻip pakutiyāy vaḻaṅkuvataṉṟit taṉittu vaḻaṅkuvataṉṟu eṉa orupaṭittāyp pakarntuviṭṭaṉar.
aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam eṉṉum coṟṟoṭarkaḷ paṇṭaik kālattil aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷamai caṅka nūlkaḷālaṟiyappaṭum. ikkālattilum cilar aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam eṉṟē kūṟuvatai ulakavaḻakkiṟ kāṇalām.
cīvaka cintāmaṇi, vimalaiyārilampakam 54 ām ceyyuḷil,

īṅkaṉañ celka tāṉeṉa eṉṉai
eṉṉum aṭiyilum, īṅkaṉam eṉṟa vaṭivam vantuḷḷatu.
iṅkaṉam eṉiṉum īṅkaṉam eṉiṉum oṉṟē. cuṭṭum viṉāvum nīḷvatu iruvakai vaḻakkiṉum perumpāṉmai. allatu cuṭṭuviṉāp poruṇmaiyil oru kuṟilum ataṉ neṭilum ōreḻuttē yeṉak koḷḷiṉum amaiyum. iṉi, aṅkaṉam, iṅkaṉam eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mutal vaṭivutāṉ yātōveṉiṟ kūṟutum.
kaṇ eṉṉum iṭappeyar cuṭṭu viṉāvoṭu puṇarntu. ak kaṇ, ikkaṇ, ekkaṇ eṉa niṟkum. piṉpu melittaṉ muṟaiyāl aṅkaṇ, iṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu:  kaṭuntēr kuḻitta ñeḷḷal āṅkaṇ  (puṟam. 15)
aṅkaṇ eṉiṉum āṅkaṇ eṉiṉum oṉṟē. muṉṉataṉ nīṭci piṉṉateṉiṉum okkum. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
aṅkaṇ, iṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa aṅkaṉ, iṅkaṉ eṉa maruvi mīṇṭum aṅṅaṉ, iṅṅaṉ eṉa meliyum.
e-ṭu: tīrttat tuṟaipaṭivē ṉeṉṟavaṉaip pērttiṅṅaṉ  (cilap.)
tamiḻccoṟkaḷ pala iṉṉōcai paṟṟi ammīṟu peṟutal perum pāṉmai. (iv viyalpai ilattīṉ moḻiyilum kāṇalām).
e-ṭu: tūṇ - tūṇam 	kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam.
	kāl - kālam	neñcu - neñcam
	puṟavu - puṟavam	kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam.
iṅṅaṉamē aṅṅaṉ, iṅṅaṉ mutaliya marūuc coṟṟoṭar kaḷum ammīṟu peṟṟu aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
ōriṭam maṟṟōriṭattiṟku vaḻiyāyiruttalpaṟṟip potuvāy iṭappeyarkaḷ oru viṉai ceyyum vaḻiyai atāvatu vakaiyaik kuṟippatuṇṭu.

	avvaḻi 	= appaṭi.

e-ṭu: ippaṭip pō = ivvaḻiyāyp pō.
aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliya peyarkaḷum coṟpaṭi mutalil iṭattaik kuṟittaṉavēṉum, piṉpu iṭam - vaḻi - vakai eṉṉum iyaipupaṟṟi ākupeyart taṉmaiyil vakai, vitam eṉṉum poruḷ taruvavāyiṉa.
iṉi, aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam mutaliya coṟṟoṭart tiripukaḷ ammaṭṭilamai yātu aṉṉaṇam. iṉṉaṇam mutaliya tiripuṅ koḷḷum, naṉṉūlārē.

oṭṭi ṉavvu mutalā kummē

caṅkītam
    āciriyar	        	  vaṭanūl			  kālam
cāraṅkatēvar	caṅkīta ratṉākaram		ki.pi. 1200
akōpilar	caṅkīta pārijātam		ki.pi. 1600
vēṅkaṭamaki	caturtaṇṭi pirakācikā	ki.pi. 1637
talaikkaḻaka iṭaikkaḻakat tamiḻ icai nāṭaka nūlkaḷ aṉaittum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa.
nāratar iyaṟṟiya pañca pāratīyam, yāmaḷēntirar iyaṟṟiya intirakāḷiyam, cikaṇṭiyār iyaṟṟiya icai nuṇukkam ākiya tamiḻ icai nūlkaḷ pāratak kālattaiyaṭuttu, ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum iyaṟṟap peṟṟavaiyākum.
ki.pi. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai pāṇaruṅ kūttarumē tamiḻakattil iṉṉicaiyum nāṭakamum payiṟṟiyum vaḷarttum vantārēṉum, pirāmaṇar kaḻakak kālattilēyē muṉṉōkkoṭu avviru kalaikaḷaiyum payiṉṟum avai paṟṟiya nūlkaḷai vaṭamoḻiyiṟ peyarttum vaittiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
āriyar tamiḻ nūlkaḷai vaṭamoḻiyil moḻipeyarkkum pōtu, peyarpput tōṉṟāvāṟu cila vaḻikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭi rukkiṉṟaṉar. avai, maṟu pākupāṭu (Re-classification), putup peyarīṭu (Change of nomenclature), māṟṟam (Alteration), cērkkai (Addition) eṉpaṉa. itu icainūliṟ piṟaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻp pāṇar, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum vīṇai vāyilāka icai nuṭpaṅkaḷaiyellām tam eḵkuc cevi yālum kūrmatiyāluṅ kaṇṭaṟintu; paṇṇum paṇṇiyalum tiṟamum tiṟattiṟamumāka nālvakaip paṇkaḷ vakuttu, nāṟperum paṇkaḷiṉiṉṟu nūṟṟumūṉṟum paṉṉīrāyiramumāka (11991) narappaṭaivāṟ paṇperukki; āyappālai vaṭṭappālai caturappālai tirikōṇappālai eṉṉum paṇtirivu muṟai kaḷāl pēricaikaḷ araiyuṅ kālum araikkālumāṉaciṟṟicai kaḷāka nuṇukum vakai nuṇittaṟintu; peṇṇiṟkup piṉ paṇ eṉṉu māṟu ellaiyaṟṟa iṉpam payakkum muṟaiyil vaḷart tirunta icaittamiḻ eṉṉum tamiḻicaiyaik karunāṭaka caṅkītam eṉap peyar māṟṟi, pāṇarait tīṇṭātavareṉa vilakki, paṉṉīricaik kōvaiyaip patiṉāṟicaik kōvai yeṉak kāṭṭi, ākaṇavuḻai (cutta mattimam) antaravuḻai (piratimat timam) eṉṉum icai vēṟupaṭṭāl eḻupattiru tāyp paṇṇeṉa vakuttu; āmpal, kuṟiñci, curuṭṭi, cevvaḻi, takkam, nāṭṭai, nēricai, puṟanīrmai, mullai, viḷari eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷai māṟṟik karakarap piriyā, caṅkarā paraṇam, tanyāci, nīlāmpari, pairavi, mattiyamāvati, vaṉajāṭci eṉa āriya iṭukuṟip peyar kaḷaiyiṭṭu, vaṭa moḻiyilum vaṭamoḻi kalanta tiraviṭattilum pāṭṭukkaḷai iyaṟṟippāṭi, āriyak kalaiyākak kāṭṭiyiruppatu ettuṇai yiraṇṭakac ceyal!
iṅṅaṉamē tamiḻ mutaṉūlkaḷaiyoṭṭiya kaṇitam, maruttuvam mutaliya pala nūlkaḷum. kāma cūttiram, kokkōkam mutaliyaṉa vum piṟavum, vevvēṟu kālattu vaṭamoḻiyil iyaṟṟap paṭṭaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kalaikaḷaiyellām 64 āka eṇṇiṉar. nālvētam, āṟucātiram, patiṉeṇpurāṇam, aṟupattu nāṉku kalaiñāṉam eṉṟu colvatu marapu. kalaikaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭ cila, kalaikaḷalla.
vaṭamoḻi pēccumoḻi yaṉṟāyiṉum, pirāmaṇar tam kūṭṭaraviyal mēmpāṭṭaip pōṟṟik kākka, iṉṟum atil nūliyaṟṟiyum kaṭṭurai varaintum tirumukam viṭuttum varavēṟ pitaḻ paṭittuk koṭuttum varuvatu, avaratu oppuyarvaṟṟa moḻiveṟiyai viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
vaṭanūlkaḷ tōṉṟat tōṉṟat teṉṉūlkaḷ maṟaintu koṇṭē vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉ moḻit toṉṉūlellām iṟantupaṭṭapiṉ, vaṭamoḻip peyarppu nūlum putunūlum, mutunūlum mūla nūlumpōṟ kāṭciyaḷik kiṉṟaṉa. itu, katiravaṉ karantapiṉ tiṅkaḷ tikaḻva tottatē.
vaṭamoḻi tamiḻakattil tamiḻālēyē vaḷarntateṉpatai, vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷum viyākaraṇaṅkaḷum icainaṭa kaṇiya maruttuva nūlkaḷum, civa tirumāl neṟikaḷum maṭṭumaṉṟi, ciṟanta vaṭa nūlāciriyar palar tamiḻ nāṭṭārāyiruntatum, 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vētaṅkaṭku viḷakkavurai varainta cāyaṉāc cāriyār teṉṉāṭṭārāyiruntatum, iṉṟum talaiciṟanta vaṭamoḻip paṇṭitar tamiḻ nāṭṭārāyiruppatum, teḷḷat teḷiyak kāṭṭum.
vaṭamoḻip paṭṭāṅku nūlkaḷil, peruntoṭariyam (mahā vākya) eṉap paṟaicāṟṟappeṟum tat tvam asi eṉṉum coṟṟoṭarē, muṟṟun tamiḻttiripāyiruntu vaṭamoḻi yāḷarait talaikaviḻac ceykiṉṟatu.
tāṉ - tat, nūm - num - tum - tvam, iru - i (tiyūt) - e (ilat, ki.) - a (va.). ti (muṉṉilaiyorumaiyīṟu) - si.
tāṉ eṉpatu mutaṟkālattiṟ paṭarkkaiyorumaic cuṭṭup peyarāyiruntu, piṉpu taṟcuṭṭup peyarāyiṟṟu.
caccari
caccari - jarjarā (jh)
itu teṉṉāṭṭup paṟaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
kokkaraiyiṉ caccariyiṉ pāṇiyāṉai  (tēvā. 722:1)
ippeyar olikkuṟippaṭippaṭaiyil tōṉṟiyatu.  (va.va. 137.)
caṭam - jaṭa
vaṭavar kuḷirmai eṉpatai mūlakkaruttāka vaittu, kuḷir, cilleṉal, viṟaippu, marappu. acaiviṉmai, uṇarviṉmai, uṇarcciyiṉmai, mayakkam, timir, maṭamai, mantam, uyiriṉmai, aṟiviṉmai eṉṟu muṟaiyē poruḷ vaḷarppar. aṟiñar itaṉ poruntāmaiyai aṟintukoḷka.
kuḷirmaip poruḷil jaṭa eṉpatu caḷi eṉṉum teṉcol tiripāka irukkalām.
caṭam - caṭalam - uṭampu. itu ulaka vaḻakku.
x.neh.: paṭam - paṭalam.
caṭalam eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
caṭalam - cataram = uṭampu (nellai vaḻakku).
cataram - catiram (koccait tiripu)  (va.va. 140-141.)
caṭu kuṭu viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭṭiṉ peyar: āṭuvāruḷ ovvoruvaṉum etirkkaṭciyiṉ ellaikkuṭ ceṉṟu, caṭukuṭu eṉṟu colliyāṭum āṭṭu, caṭukuṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. itu, pāṇṭi nāṭṭil kuṭaṭṭi eṉṟum, vaṭacōḻa nāṭṭil paliccappiḷāṉ allatu palīṉ caṭukuṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟum. caṭukuṭu eṉpatu teṉcōḻanāṭṭilum koṅku nāṭṭilum itaṟku vaḻaṅkum peyarām.
āṭuvār tokai : nālvarkkuk kuṟaiyāta iraṭṭaip paṭaiyāṉa tokaiyiṉar, itai āṭalām.
āṭiṭam : veḷinilattilum, poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟai : āṭuvārellārum, uttikaṭṭic camat tokaiyāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoḷvar. iru kaṭciyār iṭattiṟkum iṭaiyil oru kuṟukkuk kōṭu kīṟappeṟum, cōḻa nāṭṭār itai uppukkōṭu eṉpar.
mutalāvatu, entak kaṭciyār pāṭivaruvatu eṉṟu ētāvatoru vakaiyil tīrmāṉikkappeṟum. aṅṅaṉam tīrmāṉikkappeṟṟa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ uppukkōṭṭait tāṇṭi etirkkaṭci yellaikkuṭ pukuntu, caṭukuṭu caṭukuṭu eṉṟō, caṭukuṭu kuṭu kuṭu kuṭu eṉṟō, palīṉ caṭukuṭu kuṭukuṭu eṉṟō, iṭai viṭātu pāṭikkoṇṭu etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉaiyō, palaraiyō toṭṭu viṭṭu avaṉiṭamēṉum avariṭamēṉum piṭikoṭātu taṉ kaṭci ellaikkuḷ vantuviṭa vēṇṭum. aṅ ṅaṉam varumvarai pāṭuvatai niṟuttakkūṭātu. avaṉāl toṭap paṭṭava rellām tolaintuviṭuvar. avar āṭṭiṟkalavātu oru puṟamāyp pōyiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṉ, toṭap paṭṭa kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉ iṅṅaṉamē etirkkaṭci yellaik kuṭ pukuntu āṭic cella vēṇṭum.
oruvaṉ etirkkaṭci ellaikkuḷ āṭumpōtu pāṭuvatait tāṉē niṟuttiviṭṭālum, etirkkaṭciyāriṭam piṭipaṭṭu atai niṟuttiviṭṭālum, avaṉ tolaintavaṉāvaṉ. tolai talaip paṭṭup pōtal eṉpar. oru kaṭciyār tam ellaikkuḷ āṭum etiriyait toṭṭup piṭikka muyaṉṟu muṭiyāmaṟpōyiṉ, avaṉait toṭṭavarellām toṭappaṭṭarākavē karutap paṭṭut tolaintu viṭuvar. oruvaṉ etirkkaṭciyāruḷ oru vaṉaiyō palaraiyō toṭṭuviṭṭu varumpōtu, taṉ kaṭci yellaikkuṭ kālvaikkumuṉ pāṭuvatai niṟuttiviṭiṉ avaṉāl toṭappaṭṭavar paṭṭuppōkār.
taṉippaṭṭavarkku veṟṟiyō tōlviyō illāmal iru kaṭciyārum camamākavuḷḷavarai, kaṭcik koruvaṉāka māṟi māṟip pāṭivaral vēṇṭum. oru kaṭciyār oruvaṉai iḻantu viṭiṉ (aḵtāvatu avaruḷ oruvaṉ paṭṭuppōṉāl) avarē aṭutta muṟaiyum pāṭic cella vēṇṭum.
orucila āṭakar caṭukuṭu eṉṟu collāmal pāṭṭup pōl mōṉai yetukai amainta neṭumoḻit toṭarkaḷaiyum colvatuṇṭu. (oruvaṉ taṉ veṟṟi vīram valimai mutaliya vaṟṟai etiriyiṭam mikuttuk kūṟuvatu neṭumoḻiyām).
caṭukuṭu mutaliya toṭarkaḷai mūccuviṭāmaṟ collavēṇṭum eṉpatu marapu. āyiṉum cilar mūccu viṭṭuk koṇṭē iṭaiviṭātu pāṭikkoḷvar.
entak kaṭciyil aṉaivarum muṉpu paṭukiṉṟaṉarō, antak kaṭci tōṟṟatākum. tōṟṟavarkkut tōlviyāl uṇṭākum ciṟṟavamāṉamēyaṉṟi vēṟoru taṇṭaṉaiyumillai.
āṭṭuttōṟṟa viḷakkam: paṇṭait tamiḻaracar ceytu vanta pōrvakaikaḷuḷ veṭci eṉpatu oṉṟu. atu nirai kavartal allatu nirai mīṭṭal. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉatu karantai yeṉappaṭum.
kuṟumparacar koḷḷait talaivar ceyalākavāvatu, vēntar ēvalālāvatu nikaḻvatu nirai kavartal. nirai kavarappaṭṭa nāṭṭaracaṉ taṉ paṭaikaḷaik koṇṭu nirai mīṭṭal karantai.
nirai kavarumpōtum nirai mīṭkumpōtum pōr nikaḻum. ap pōril veṟṟi tarumāṟu koṟṟavai eṉṉum kāḷikkut tuṭi koṭṭip paliyiṭutal marapu. itu koṟṟavai nilai yeṉap paṭum.
maṟaṅkaṭaik kūṭṭiya tuṭinilai ciṟanta
koṟṟavai nilaiyum attiṇaip puṟaṉē  (puṟat. 4)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
caṭukuṭu eṉṉum viḷaiyāṭṭu, veṭcip pōriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya tākat terikiṉṟatu. iru kaṭciyārum veṭci maṟavarum karantai maṟavarum pōlvar. iṭaikkōṭu, avvirucārār nāṭṭiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa ellaippuṟam pōṉṟatu. palicaṭukuṭu eṉṟu pāṭuvatu, kāḷikkup paliyiṭuvataiyum tuṭi koṭṭuvataiyum kuṟippatu (tuṭippaṟaiyiṟ ciṟiyavakai caṭukuṭukkai allatu kuṭuku ṭuppai eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.) irukaṭciyārum tattam etirk kaṭciyāraip piṭittu niṟuttuvatu, nirai kavar talaiyum nirai mīṭṭalaiyum nikarppatu. paṭṭuppōtal eṉṉum vaḻakku veṭcippōril vīḻntiṟattalaik kuṟippatu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : inta āṭṭiṉāl, nīṇṭa nēram mūccaṭakkavum, tiruṭaṉaip piṭikkavum, etiriyiṉiṉṟu tappik koḷḷavum, payiṟci peṟalām.
koṅkunāṭṭu vēṟupāṭu : pāṇṭināṭṭār caṭukuṭuviṟku iṭaikkō ṭoṉṟē kīṟikkoḷvar. koṅku nāṭṭārō, irukaṭciyārkkum iru vēṟu caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷai amaittuk koḷvar. āṭumpōtu ovvoruvarum tattam kaṭṭattiṟ kuḷḷēyē niṟṟal vēṇṭum. veḷiyēṟiṉ vilakiviṭal vēṇṭum.

cataram
cataram - caturacra (u,ḷaÝ)
caṭṭutal = taṭṭutal. ivviṉai iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟatu. āyiṉum caṭṭuccaṭṭeṉṟu taṭṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkuḷḷatu.
caṭṭeṉṟu = caṭṭeṉṟu taṭṭumaḷavil, caṭṭeṉṉum ōcai kēṭkumaḷavil, viraintu.
caṭṭu - caṭṭam = taṭṭaiyāṉa palakai. oru poruḷait taṭṭat taṭṭa mēṉmēlun taṭṭaiyātal kāṇka. itaṉāl taṭṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu taṭṭaik karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
x.neh.: taṭṭu - taṭṭam, taṭṭai; paṭṭu - paṭṭam, paṭṭai; moṭṭu - maṭṭu - maṭṭai.
caṭṭam = 1. palakai ōlai mutaliya taṭṭaiyāṉa poruḷ. 
2. kottaṉ caṭṭap palakai, 3. caṭṭap palakai pōṉṟa nērmai, 4. variyiḻukkuñ caṭṭam (Flat ruler) 5. nērvari, 6. nērvari pōṉṟa oḻukkaneṟi, caṭṭa tiṭṭam.
7. nāṟpuṟamun taitta caṭṭam (Frame), nāṟpuṟac caṭṭamāṉa caturam;
8. uyirukku allatu ātaṉukku (āṉmāviṟku)c caṭṭam pōṉṟirukkum uṭampu.
(āṅkilattilum caṭṭattaik kuṟikkum (kacayaana) eṉṉuñ col uṭampaik kuṟittal kāṇka.);
9. kaṭṭil mutaliyaṉa piṉṉutaṟkum vīṭu kaṭṭutaṟkum paṭam varaitaṟkum amaittuk koḷḷum caṭṭa amaippu, 
10. muṉṉēṟpāṭāṉa tiṭṭam (Plan), 11. aṇiyam (āyattam);
12. kaṭṭurai mutaliyaṉa varaitaṟkuk kuṟittuk koḷḷum kuṟippu;
13. pārtteḻututaṟku vaittuk koḷḷum mēlvariyeḻuttu, 14. etaṟkum aḷavaiyāṉa mēlvaric caṭṭam, 15. ceppam allatu cīrmai.
caṭṭam - caṭṭakam = 1. caṭṭam (Frame).

2. kaṭṭil, paṭukkai (tivā.)
3. vaṭivu (piṅ.). 4. uṭal.
uyirpukuñ caṭṭakam (kallā. 8:1).
caṭṭam, caṭṭakam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyil illai.
caṭṭam - caṭam = 1. uṭampu. x.neh.: paṭṭam - paṭam = tuṇi.
caṭaṅkoḷ cīvarap pōrvaiyar  (tēvā. 805:10).
2. uṭampupōl aṟivillāp poruḷ (piṅ).  (va.va. 140.)
cataram (2) - carīra (i.vē.).
ta-ra. pōli. x.neh.: vitai-virai.
vaṭavaruḷ oru cārār cri eṉṉum collaik kāṭṭit tāṅku vatu eṉṟum, maṟṟoru cārār crū eṉṉuñ collaikkāṭṭi eḷitāy aḻikkappaṭuvatu eṉṟum, poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvar.
cataram - caturam = nāṉ mūlaic caṭṭampōṉṟa nāṟkōṇam. 
vaṭṭamuñ caturamum  (peruṅ. uñcaik 42: 29).
caturakkal, caturakkaḷḷi, caturat tūṇ, caturap palakai, caturap pālai, catura māṭam, catura mukam, catura varam, catura vōṭu eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakku.
vaṭṭam eṉpatu pōṉṟē caturam eṉpatum tūya teṉcol, āyiṉ, vaṭa nūlkaḷil āḷappeṟṟiruppatu paṟṟi, caturam eṉpataip pōṉṟē vaṭṭam eṉpataiyum vaṭacolleṉa valippar vaṭavar. vaṭṭam (ta.) - vaṭṭa (pirā.) - vrutta (va.) eṉṉum uṇmaiyāṉa muṟaiyait talaikīḻāyk kāṭṭuvar avar.
caturam eṉṉum vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyil illai.  (va.va. 141).
caturam - catur acra = nāṟkōṇam.
mēlaiyāriyattil nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟē caturattaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
L. quatuor = four; exquadra - OF esquarre - E square.
vaṭamoḻiyil atu piṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟuk kuṟikkiṉṟatu. catur eṉṉum peyar iyalpāka niṉṟu nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇup peyaraiyē uṇarttukiṉṟatu. caturikā, catuṣka, catuṣṭaya eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷum piṟkālattilēyē catura vaṭivaiyum uṇarttiṉatākat terikiṉṟatu.
acra = kōṇam, mūlai.
caturam eṉṉum vaṭivak karuttu nāṉmūlaic caṭṭattoṭu maṭṭumaṉṟi, kaṭṭuṭampoṭum toṭarpuṭaiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
kaṭṭuṭampiṉ amaippaic caturkkaṭṭu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. āṅkilattilum square - built, a man of square frame eṉṟu vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka;
caṭalam, cataram eṉṉum col vaṭivaṅkaḷ kumarikkaṇṭattil caṭṭak karuttum uṇarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
catura eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu, tōṉṟiya caturi eṉṉum viṉai vaṭamoḻiyilillai.
caturittal = nāṟkōṇamākkutal.
kuḻittal (varukkamākkutal). cariyāṉa caturattaik kuṟikkum caccatu ram eṉṉum iraṭṭitta vaṭivam vaṭamoḻiyilillai.  (va.va.142.)
