campaḷam
paḻaṅkālattil campaḷam kūlamum uppumākak koṭukkappaṭ ṭatu. kūlam - tāṉiyam. (kūlamākak koṭukkappaṭuvatu kūli) kūlattil ciṟantatu nellātaliṉ, nel vakaiyil ciṟanta campāviṉ peyarālum, uppiṉ peyarālum campaḷam eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
campum aḷamum cērntatu campaḷam. campu eṉpatu ciṟanta nel vakaikkum ciṟanta kōrai vakaikkum potuppeyar. ōṅki vaḷarnta campā neṟpayirukkum campaṅkōraikkum otta tōṟṟa muṭaimaiyāy iruttal kāṇka. nellaik kuṟikkum campu eṉṉum peyar iṉṟu campā eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. 
ukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ ākāra īṟu peṟuvatu iyalpē. e-ṭu: kumpu - kumpā, tūmpu - tūmpā, kuṇṭu - kuṇṭā. kumputal tiraḷutal.
campaḷam
aḷam eṉpatu uppu. uppu viḷaippōr aḷavar eṉappaṭutal kāṇka. aḷam eṉṉum col taṉ poruḷ iḻantu īṟāka māṟiya piṉ uppaik kuṟikka uppaḷam eṉṟoru col vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. uppaic campaḷap pakutiyākak koṭukkum vaḻakkam niṉṟu viṭavē umpaḷam eṉṉum collum apporuḷil vaḻakku vīḻntu māṉiyamākak koṭukkum nilattiṟkup peyarāka āḷappaṭṭatu. umpu - uppu.
uppukku uḻaittal uppait tiṉṉutal eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷaiyum uppiṭṭavarai uḷḷavum niṉai eṉṟum paḻamoḻi yaiyum nōkkuka.  (col. 22, 23.)
(Salary (Soldier’s pay. which was given partly in Salt - wages English word book p.90)]  (col. 22).
camam
camam - sama = oppu.
um - am - cam - camam = oppu. camam - camaṉ = oppu, naṭunilai.  (va.va.142.)
camayam
matam, camayam eṉṉum iru collum oru poruḷaṉavāyi ṉum, tammuḷ nuṇ poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṭaiyaṉa. oru teyvattai allatu iṟaivaṉai matittu vaḻipaṭuvatu matam; iṟaivaṉai immaiyilēṉum maṟumaiyilēṉum aṭaivataṟkuc camaivatu camayam. ākavē, matattiṉum camayattiṟku oḻukkamum nōṉpum mika vēṇṭuvaṉavām.
uttal = poruntutal, utti (u+ti) = poruttam, nūṟkup poruntum muṟai, viḷaiyāṭṭiṟ kaṉṉai (kaṭci) pirikka iruvar iṇaital.
uttam = poruttam. uttam - va. yukta.
utti - va. yukti.
u - o. ottal = poruntutal, pōlutal.
u - um. ummutal = poruntutal, kūṭutal.
eṇṇummai yiṭaiccollāṉatu. ituvē,
um - am = poruntum nīr, nīroṭu nīrum nilattoṭu nīrum ākap poruntutal iruvakaittu.
am - amar. amartal = poruntutal, aṉpāl uḷḷam oṉṟutal.
am - amai. amaital = poruntutal, aṭaṅkiyiruttal, kūṭutal, neruṅkutal, nukarccik kēṟṟatātal, takkatāka vāyttal.
amai - avai = kūṭṭam.
amai - amaiyam = poruntum nēram, ēṟṟa vēḷai, takka cevvi, viṉai nikaḻum ciṟupoḻutu.
amai - camai. camaital = ākkappaṭum arici pōlum pūp paṭaiyum kaṉṉi pōlum nukarccik kēṟṟatātal, pataṉaṭaital, iṉitākac ceyporuḷ viṉai muṭital, aṇiyamātal.
camaiyal = cōṟu kuḻampākkum viṉai.
cakkiliyap peṇṇum cāmaik katirum camaintāl teriyum  (paḻamoḻi)
camai - camaiyam - pataṉaṭainta allatu nukarccik kēṟṟa nilai, viṉaikkut takka vēḷai.
camaiyam - camayam - ātaṉ (āṉmā) tāṉ iṟaivaṉai aṭai taṟku allatu pēriṉpattai nukartaṟkut taṉṉait takutippaṭut tum mukkaraṇa voḻukkam.
poruḷai vēṟupaṭuttaṟku, colliṭai makara aikāṉ akara māyiṟṟeṉa aṟika.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, camayam eṉṉum teṉcollai samaya eṉṟu tirittum, sam + aya eṉṟu pirittum, uṭaṉvarutal (to come together) allatu oṉṟu cērtal eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. aṅṅaṉaṅ kāṭṭiya piṉpum,
ūḻiṟ peruvali yāvuḷa maṟṟoṉṟu
cūḻiṉun tāṉmun tuṟum.  (kuṟaḷ. 380)
eṉṉun tiruvaḷḷuvar tiruvāymoḻik kēṟpa, avvaṭacol teṉcoṟ ṟiripēyātal kāṇka.
 sam = uṭaṉ, kūṭa. aya = celkai, varukai.
aya eṉṉum coṟku, ay eṉpatai aṭiyākavum i eṉpatai vērākavum kāṭṭuvar. i - ay - ayaṉa = celavu.
tamiḻil, kūṭutalaik kuṟikkum aṭiccoṟkaḷuḷ kum eṉpatu oṉṟu. kummutal = kūṭutal, kuvital.
kum - kummal. kum - kumpu - kumpal.
kum - kumi - kumiyal. kumi - kuvi - kuviyal.
kummal - L. cumulus = heap; cumulare = to heap up. E. cumulate, accumulate.
kum - kumma (nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccam - Infinitive Mood) - kūṭa. kūṭutal = oṉṟu cērtal, mikutal.
kūṭa (to gather) eṉṉum (ni.kā.) viṉaiyeccam, uṭaṉ (together, with) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumai uṭaṉikaḻcci yurupākavum, muṉṉoṭ ṭākavum (prefix) āḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu: 	tantaikūṭa vantāṉ = tantaiyuṭaṉ vantāṉ.
		kūṭappiṟantāṉ = uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ.
kūṭutalaik kuṟikkum kum eṉṉum viṉaiccol, u-a tiripu muṟaippaṭi kam eṉṟu tiriyavuñ ceyyum.
kamam niṟait tiyalum  (tol. uri. 57)
niṟaital - niramputal, mikutal, kūṭutal.
Cum eṉṉum ilattīṉa aṭic collum, muṉṉoṭṭākum pōtu com eṉṟu tirintu, varuñcoṉ mutaleḻuttiṟkēṟpa con, col, cor eṉṟu īṟumāṟum. cila eḻuttukkaṭku muṉ co eṉṟu īṟu keṭavuñ ceyyum. iṅṅaṉamē āṅkilattilum.
iṅṅaṉam ilattīṉattil kum-kom eṉṟum, āṅkilattiṟ kam eṉṟum tiriyum muṉṉoṭṭu, kirēkkattil sum (sum) eṉṟum sim (sym) eṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil sam (sam) eṉṟum, tiriyum. kirēkka muṉṉoṭṭum, ilattīṉa muṉṉoṭṭup pōṟ piṉvarum eḻuttiṟkēṟpa, ciṉ (syn), cil (syl) eṉṟum, tiriyum. camaṟkiruta muṉṉoṭṭu īṟukeṭṭu sa (sa) eṉṟum niṟkum.
ivaṟṟiṟ kellām, kūṭa (uṭaṉ) eṉpatē poruḷ.
kūṭa eṉpataṟku nērāṉa kumma eṉṉum teṉcollē, muṉṉoṭṭākik kum eṉṟu kuṟukiyum, kum - kom - kam, kum - sum, sum - sam eṉṟu tirintum, āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum eṉa aṟika. ataṉ mutaṉmey, airōppāviṉ vaṭamēṟkup pakutiyilum mēṟkup pakutiyilum iyalpākavum, ataṉ teṉ kiḻakkuppakutiyilum intiyāvilum sakaramākat tirintum, vaḻaṅkuvatai ūṉṟi nōkki varalāṟṟuṇmai teḷika.
iṉi, intiya āriyattil (vētamoḻiyilum camaṟkirutattilum) cellutalaik kuṟikkum i eṉṉum vērum ay eṉṉum aṭiyumākiya viṉaiccoṟkaḷum, uy eṉṉum teṉcolli ṉiṉṟu tirintavaiyē.
ū allatu u, itaḻkuvintolittu muṉṉiṭattai allatu muṉṉiṟpataic cuṭṭum muṉmaic cuṭṭu.
ū-ūṉ = nī (muṉṉilai yorumaip peyar).
ū - u - untu. untutal = muṉ taḷḷutal.
u - uy. uytal = muṉ cellutal, cellutal.
uyttal = muṉceluttutal, celuttutal.
uy - iy - iyavu = ka. celavu.
iṭaineṟik kiṭanta iyavukkoṇ maruṅkil  (cilap. 11: 168) 2 vaḻi.
iyavu - iyavuḷ - ellā vuyirkaḷaiyum vaḻinaṭattum iṟaivaṉ.
iy - i - iyal - celavu. puḷḷiyaṟ kalimā  (tol. po. 196).
iyalutal - cellutal, naṭattal.
arivaiyoṭu meṉmela viyali  (aiṅ. 175)
iya - iyaṅku - iyakku - iyakkam. iyaṅkutal - cellutal, naṭattal, acaital.
iy - va. ay. iṉi, uy eṉpatum ay eṉṟu tirintirukkalām.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku muntiyatu eṉṉum varalāṟṟuṇmaiyaiyum, mēṟkāṭṭiya col varalāṟukaḷaiyum, naṭunilaiyāka nōkku vārkku, matam camayam eṉṉum irucollum teṉ collē yeṉpatu, teṟṟeṉat teriyāmaṟ pōkātu.  (ta.ma.)
camayamum koḷkaiyum
immai maṟumai nalaṅkaṭkum vīṭṭiṉpattiṟkum iṟaivaṉaruḷ iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉṉuṅ koḷkai paṟṟi, paṇṭait tamiḻaracar tamakkum tamkuṭikaṭkum nalam varuvittaṟ poruṭṭut, tamakkicaintatoru camayattaik kaṭaippiṭittu iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.
tamiḻaracaruṅ kuṭikaḷum mutalāvatu aintiṇait teyvaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. kuṟiñcikkuc cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉum, mullaikku māyōṉ eṉṉun tirumālum, marutattiṟku vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉum, neytaṟku varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) eṉṉum kaṭalōṉum, pālaikku māyōḷ eṉṉuṅ kāḷi (koṟṟavai) yum, teyvamāyiruntaṉar.
varuṇa vaṇakkam nāḷaṭaivil niṉṟuviṭṭatu. intira vaṇakkam kaṭaiccaṅkat tiṟutivaraiyiruntatu. maṟṟa mutteyva vaṇakkamum neṭukalun toṭarntatu.
kaṭaiccaṅka kālattil, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattilum maturai yilum vañciyilum paṟpala teyvaṅkaṭkuk kōyil iruntaṉa. pukārilirunta kōyilkaḷ, kaṟpakak kōyil, veḷyāṉaik kōyil, palatēvar kōyil, katiravaṉ kōyil, kayilaikkōyil, murukaṉ kōyil, vaccirap paṭaikkōyil, cāttaṉ kōyil, arukaṉkōyil, nilāk kōyil, civaṉkōyil, tirumāl kōyil mutaliyaṉa. murukaṉ civaṉ tirumāl palatēvaṉ ākiya, nālvarkkum ellāt talainakarkaḷilum kōyil iruntaṉa.
teyvaṅkaḷ perunteyvam eṉṟum ciṟu teyvam eṉṟum iruvakaippaṭum. camayat teyvaṅkaḷ perun teyvamum maṟṟavai ciṟuteyvamumākum. caivam vaiṇavam ārukatam pauttam piramam ulakāyatam eṉṉum āṟum, kaṭaiccaṅka kālattu aṟucamayam eṉṉalām. akam akappuṟam puṟam puṟappuṟam eṉa nālāṟu camayamākap pakuttuk kūṟiyatu piṟkālatta tākum. civavaḻipāṭum mālvaḻipāṭum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuriya vātaliṉ, avviraṇṭum paṟṟiya caivamum vaiṇavamumē tamiḻaracarāl perumpālum kaṭaippiṭikkap paṭṭaṉa. avviraṇṭaṉuḷḷum civaneṟiyē aracariṭaic ciṟantiruntatu. iṟaivaṉ eṉṟum piṟavāyākkaip periyōṉ eṉṟum civaṉukku vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷē, civaneṟit talaimaiyai uṇarttiviṭum.
ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷ, kāṟṟum neruppum mutaliya iyaṟkait teyvamum, cūraramakaḷum pūtamum mutaliya pēyt teyvamum, katiravaṉun tiṅkaḷum mutaliya kōḷ teyvamum, paṭṭamaṟava ṉākiya naṭukalteyvamum, iṟanta kaṟpiyākiya pattiṉit teyvamum, nālvakai varuṇattiṟkuriya varuṇatteyvamum, āṟum, malaiyum nakarum mutaliya iṭattiṟkuriya iṭatteyvamum, kalviyum kātalum pōrum mutaliya toḻiṟkuriya toḻiṟ ṟeyvamum, kāppumaṭṭum aḷippatākak karutappaṭum kāpput teyvamum eṉap palavakaiyām.
kaṭaiccaṅkak kālamvarai, aracarum pulavarum potu makkaḷu mākiya muttiṟattāriṭaiyum, camaviyaṟ koḷkaiyum piṟar matap poṟaiyum iruntuvantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ camayap pōr tamiḻnāṭ ṭil talai tūkkiṟṟu. mutalāvatu, vaitika camayam eṉappaṭum caiva vaiṇavaṅkaṭkum avaitika camayam eṉappaṭum camaṇa pauttaṅkaṭkum iṭaiyē pōr eḻuntatu. piṉṉarc camaṇa pauttaṅkaḷ oṭukkappaṭṭapiṉ, caiva vaiṇavaṅkaṭ kiṭaiyē pōr mūṇṭatu.
caṅkakālattaracarum mutalām irācarācaṉ irācēntiraṉ mutaliya piṟkālattaracarum, tām kaṭaippiṭitta camayak kōyil kaṭku maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟa matak kōyil kaṭkum māṉiyam viṭṭa ṉar. āyiṉ 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum camaṇaṉumāṉa makēntira varmap pallavaṉ caiva camaya kuravaruḷ oruvarāṉa tirunāvuk karacaraip palavakaiyil vataittuk kolla muyaṉṟāṉ. atē nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum civaṉaṭiyāruḷ oruvaṉumāṉa neṭumāṟaṉ eṇṇāyiram camaṇaraik kaḻuvēṟṟiṉāṉ. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṉaṉum caivaṉu māṉa 2 ām kulōttuṅkaṉ, kūrattāḻvāṉ periyanampi eṉṉum iru vaiṇava kuravarai muṟaiyē kaṇ paṟittuc cākaṭittāṉ; atōṭu tillai maṉṟilirunta tirumā luruvattaiyum peyarttuk  kaṭalil eṟintuviṭṭāṉ. iṅṅaṉam oru cila aracar neṟi tiṟampi naṭantaṉar. tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār nākaiyilirunta poṟputta vuruvaik koḷḷaiyaṭittut, tiruvaraṅkak kōyilaip putup pittāreṉṟu, oru paravai vaḻakkuc ceyti vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ma.)

camarttu
camarttu - samartta (th)
vaṭavar sam + artta eṉṟu pakuttu, otta allatu takunta nōk kaṅ koḷḷutal, takunta nilaimai peṟutal, valimaiyaṭaital eṉṟu poruḷ poruttuvar.
sam = sama. art = aṭai, peṟu.  (va.va.143).
camaram
camaram - samara = pōr iruvar allatu iru paṭaikaḷ ceyyum pōtu oṉṟu kalattalāl, kalattal allatu oṉṟātaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu pōrk karuttup piṟantatu.
e-ṭu: kala - kalām, kalakam. kaikalattal = caṇṭaiyiṭutal. poru tal - ottal, pōr ceytal. poru - pōr = poruttu, camar.
iṅṅaṉamē oppaik kuṟikkum camam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum pōraik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
camam = pōr. oḷiṟuvā ḷaruñcama murukki (puṟam. 382).
camam - camar = pōr (tivā.) o.nō: kaḷam - kaḷar.
camarttal = porutal. camarkka vallāyēl (vināyakapu. 74:249)
pōrukku valimai vēṇṭumātaliṉ, pōreṉṉum col valimai allatu tiṟamaiyaiyum uṇarttum.
pōr = valimai. camarttu = tiṟamai.
camar - camaram = pōr. ilaṅkaiyi leḻunta camaramum  (cilap. 26: 238).
camar - amar = pōr. āramar ōṭṭalum  (tol. 1006).
camaram - amaram = pōr. te. amaramu.
amarttal = māṟupaṭutal. pētaik kamarttaṉa kaṇ  (kuṟaḷ. 1084).
vaṭamoḻiyil camara eṉṉum vaṭivē uḷḷatu. camam, camar, amar, amaram eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ illai.
vaṭavar samara eṉṉum collai sam + ru eṉṟu pirittu, oṉṟu kūṭu, māṟupaṭu, pōrcey eṉṟu poruḷ kāṭṭuvar.
sam = kūṭa. ru = cēr.  (va.va.142-143).
camaṟkiruta ākkam
vēta āriyar eṉṟu collappaṭum intiya āriyar intiyāviṟ kuṭ pukuntavuṭaṉ tam (kirēkkattai yotta) moḻiyai maṟantu viṭṭaṉar. itaṟku avar ciṟutokaiyiṉarā yiruntatum paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu pōṉatumē karaṇiyam.
āriyap pūcāriyarum avaraip piṉpaṟṟiya vicuvāmittiraṉ pōṉṟa orucila nāṭṭumakkaḷum, pāṭiya mantirat tokuti eṉṉum pāṭaṟṟiraṭṭēirukku vētamām. ataṉ moḻi, maṟantupōṉa kīḻaiyāriyamum vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭamākiya pirākirutamuṅ kalanta moḻiyām. akkalappiṉāl, intiya āriya moḻi ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷaiyum iḻantatu.
āriyap pūcārikaḷāṉa pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ, vētamoḻiyuṭaṉ ērāḷamāṉa tiraviṭac coṟkaḷum tamiḻccoṟkaḷum avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirikkappaṭṭa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa putuccoṟkaḷum iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷum kalanta, kalavai moḻiyil itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷum taruma cāttiraṅkaḷum nāṭakaṅkaḷum piṟavum iyaṟṟappaṭṭa piṉṉarē, ki.mu. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭupōl camaṟkirutam eṉṉum araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭaimoḻi niṟaivāka uruvāyiṟṟu. pirākirutam (pirākruta) eṉpatu muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟum, camaṟkirutam (samkruta) eṉpatu kalantu ceyyap paṭṭatu eṉṟum, poruḷpaṭuvaṉa vākum.
intairōppiyam, vētiyam, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum mūvēṟu nilaikaḷil, āriyam tamiḻccoṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷatu. vētiyam kaṭaṉkoṇṭatu vaṭanāṭṭup pirākiruta vāyilāka veṉṟaṟika. vaṭamoḻi eṉṉum peyar vētamoḻikkum camaṟkirutattiṟkum potuvākum. paicāci, cūracēṉi, mākati eṉṟu vaṭanāṭṭil mūṉṟum, tirāviṭi eṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭil oṉṟumāka, pirākirutam nāṉkeṉa vakuttaṉar.
vētamoḻi pōṉṟē camaṟkirutamum nūṉmoḻiyākum. atu piṟantatumillai; iṟantatumillai. oru moḻikku uyir ulaka vaḻakkē. cila pala paṇṭitar pallāṇṭu varuntikkaṟṟuc camaṟ kirutattiṟ pēcuvataṉāl, atu uyirmoḻiyākiviṭātu. atu uyirmeyyumaṉṟu; cavamumaṉṟu; vallōṉ puṉainta pāvai pōṉṟatē.
ulaka vaḻakku moḻikaḷāyiruntu iṟantu pōṉavaṟṟai maṭṭumaṉṟi, ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu mēlai moḻiyaṟiñar puṉainta ecupperāntō (Esperanto), nōviyal (Novial) mutaliya ceyaṟkai moḻikaḷaik kūṭak kaṟṟup pēcakkūṭiyavar ulakiluḷḷaṉar.
maṟaiyiyalāka maṭṭumuḷḷa vētamoḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuttatu aintiram eṉṟum, maṟaiyiyalum (vaitīkamum) ulakiyalum (laukīkamum) kalanta camaṟkiruta moḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuttatu pāṇiṉīyam eṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
pāṇiṉīyam
camaṟkirutattiṉ talaiciṟanta ilakkaṇa nūlākiya pāṇiṉīyam pāṇiṉiyāl ki.mu.4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. ilak kaṇanūlai viyākaraṇam eṉpar vaṭanūlār. atu kūṟupaṭuppu (Analysis) eṉṉum poruḷatu. nan nāṉku iyalkaḷ (pātaṅkaḷ) uḷḷa eṇṇatikāraṅkaḷ (attiyāyaṅkaḷ) koṇṭatu pāṇiṉi viyākaraṇam. ataṉāl atu aṭṭāttiyāyī (aṣṭāt yāyī) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ataṉ nūṟpākkaḷ (cūttiraṅkaḷ) ēṟattāḻa 3980. annūṟku muṉ eṇṇilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟap paṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu vēta kālattateṉappaṭum aintiram.
tolkāppiyam pāṇiṉīyattiṟku munnūṟṟāṇṭu muntiyatu. ataṉālēyē, aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟu maṭṭuṅ kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyariṉ uṭaṉmāṇavarāṉa paṉampāraṉār. camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷellām pavaṇanti naṉṉūlpōl eḻuttuñ collumē kūṟuvaṉa veṉṟum, tamiḻiṟpōl oru vaḻakku moḻiyai viḷakkātu vētamum iti kāca purāṇaṅkaḷumākiya ilakkiyattiluḷḷa araic ceyaṟkai yāṉa naṭaimoḻiyaiyē (Semi-artificial literary dialect) viḷakkuvaṉa veṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. 
tolkāppiyam eḻuttuñ collum poruḷumākiya mūvatikāraṅ koṇṭatu; poruḷatikārattil, tamiḻukkē ciṟap pāṉa poruḷilakkaṇattuṭaṉ ceyyuḷiyalaiyum aṇiyiyalāṉa uvamaviyalaiyum uṭkoṇṭatu. ātalāl, pāṇiṉīyatti ṉiṉṟu tolkāppiyam tōṉṟiyatākac cilar kūṟuvatu, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟa kataiyum kaḷācceṭiyiṟ palāppaḻam paṟitta kataiyumē yākum.
ghÂÅ, “mïc©”, “UY¡”, “VX§.”, “I xs¢.”, “Aatu£.”, “y©.”, ñamaṅaṇa nam. mutaliya 14 kuṟuṅkaṇakku nūṟpākkaḷai, civaperumāṉiṉ uṭukkai yiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉa olikaḷākak kūṟi, avaṟṟiṟku makēcuvara cūttiraṅkaḷ eṉappeyariṭṭatu, intiyap paḻaṅkuṭi vāṇarai ēmāṟṟat tuṇiccaluṭaṉ ceyta paṭumōcac cūḻcciyākum.
puṇṇiya caravaṇam poruntuvi rāyiṉ
viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻunū leytuvir
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷai (11:98-9) nōkkiṉ, pāṇiṉiyiṉ ēmāṟṟai makkaḷ nampi avarilakkaṇattaiyē pōṟṟip payilumāṟu, tamiḻt toṭarpu kāṭṭum aintira viyā karaṇap paṭikaḷai yellām tokuttu, aḻakar malaiyaṭuttatum makkaḷ vaḻaṅkātatum āḻam mikkatumāṉa oru poykaikkuḷ eṟintu viṭṭatākak karuta iṭamuṇṭākiṟatu. tamiḻakattut tōṉṟiya aintiram tamiḻakattilēyē aḻiyuṇṭatu pōlum!
	camaṟkirutavākkam - eḻuttu
tamiḻeḻuttu
eḻuttu, oliyum variyum eṉa iruvaṭivuṭaiyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ uṇmaiyāṉatu oliyē. oliyē eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉap paṭṭatu. uḷḷattileḻum karuttukaḷai nēraṭiyāy aṟivikkum kuṟi oli. celippulaṉāṉa oliyaik kaṭpulaṉākkum kuṟiyē vari.
muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattil, ki.mu. aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭu kaṭku muṉpē tamiḻ tāṉē tōṉṟi.
1. acai nilai + (monosyllabic stage)
2. kūṭṭu nilai + (compounding stage)
3. paku coṉṉilai - (Inflexional stage)
4. koḷuvu nilai - (Agglutinative stage)
eṉṉum nālvakai moḻi nilaiyuṅ kaṭantu muḻu vaḷarcci yaṭaintu. ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē eḻuttum ilakkiyamum ilakkaṇamum nirampappeṟṟu. mutaṉ mutalākat teṉmaturait talaikkaḻakattil ārāyap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻolikaḷ perumpālum iyaṟkaiyāṉavai; eḷiyavai; ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉavai. potuvāka, eḻut tolikaḷ teṟkē cellac cellat tokaiyilum vaṉmai yilum kuṟaintum. vaṭakkē cellac cella avaṟṟil mikuntum uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻ valliṉa volikaḷ vaṭamoḻi valliṉa voli kaḷai nōkka melliyavai. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉavai iraṭṭittā laṉṟip piṉṉavaṟṟai vaṉmaiyil ovvā. innilaimaiyai iṟṟait teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭuppuṟap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ nāviltāṉ cevvaiyākak kāṇamuṭiyum.
1. paṭaveḻuttu (pictogram or Hieroglyph)
2. karutteḻuttu (Ideogram)
3. acaiyeḻuttu (Syllabary)
4. oliyeḻuttu (Phonemic character)
eṉṉum nālvakai varinilaiyum muṟaiyē aṭainta tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku. ki.pi. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iṟṟai nilai yilēyē yirunta teṉpatu.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttum āṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi  (98)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvālum; ki.mu. 6 ām allatu 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, uyirum uyir meyyumākiya ekara okaraṅkaḷum kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum puḷḷi peṟutalum, uṭpuḷḷi peṟṟa pakara vaṭivē makara vaṭivātalum, tavira, maṟṟaiyavellām iṟṟaiyavē yeṉpatu tolkāppiyattālum, aṟiyakkiṭak kiṉṟaṉa.
eḻuttu eḻutappaṭum karuvikkēṟpa vēṟupaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭu mātalāl, eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu ēṭṭil eḻututaṟku oru varaiveḻuttu muṟaiyum. uḷi koṇṭu cempilum kallilum cetukkaṟku oru veṭṭeḻuttu muṟaiyum, toṉṟu toṭṭuk kaiyāḷappaṭṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. tamiḻiṉ toṉmai yaiyum talaimaiyaiyum āyntaṟiyum matukaiyum naṭu nilaimai yum illā ayalār, varaiveḻuttum veṭṭe ḻuttākiya vaṭ ṭeḻuttum oṉṟē yeṉap piṟaḻavuṇarntu viṭṭaṉar.
āriyavolittiripu
tamiḻolikaḷ, mutalāvatu, teṉ tiraviṭamākiya teluṅkil vaṉmaipeṟṟu vaṭa tiraviṭattil vaḷarcciyaṭaintu. vaṭa mēlai yāriya (airōppiya) moḻikaḷil iyaṟkaiyāriya vaṭivuṟṟu. iṟutiyil kīḻaiyāriyamākiya camaṟkirutattil perumpālum ceyaṟkai vaṭivu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. itai,
centiṟatta tamiḻōcai vaṭacol lāki
eṉṉum tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār tiruneṭuntāṇṭakat toṭar iraṭṭuṟal muṟaiyiṟ kuṟippatākak koḷḷalām.
eṉṟum eṅkum piñcu muṟṟik kāyākumēyaṉṟik kāy iḷantu piñcākātu. atu pōl, iyaṟkaiyāṉa moḻi vaḷarcciyil, melloli valittu valloliyākumē yaṉṟi valloli melintu melloliyākātu. kuḻantai vāyil valloli piṟavātatu pōl, muntiyal māntaṉ vāyilum valloli piṟappatillai. paḻantamiḻaṉ cūṭu eṉṟu coṉṉa collai iṟṟait tamiḻaṉ koccai vaḻakkil jūṭu eṉṟu colvatai yum, triplus eṉṟa ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu treble eṉṉum āṅkilaccol tirintiruppataiyum nōkkuka. kna eṉṟa tiyūttāṉiyac col gno eṉṟu ilattīṉil tirintatum atuvē.
iṟṟai nilaippaṭi, valliṉa eḻuttolikaḷaiyellām.
(1) kaṭuppoli (voiceless unaspirate)
(2) urappoli (voiceless aspirate)
(3) eṭuppoli (voiced unaspirate)
(4) kaṉaippoli (voiced aspirate)
eṉa nālvakaiyāka vakukkalām. ivaṟṟuḷ, tamiḻil iruppavai yellām meṉkaṭuppolikaḷum mellolikaḷumē. ivaṟṟuḷ, piṉṉavai meṉkaṭuppolikaḷ colliṭai kaṭai taṉittum melliṉattiṉ piṉpum vantālaṉṟi nikaḻā. ilattīṉil, mūccoli cērnta puṇarmeykaḷē illai; k - yum s - um cērnta x(ks) eṉṉum puṇarmeytāṉ uṇṭu. kirēkkattil, th, ph, kh eṉṉum mūccolip puṇarmeykaḷ mūṉṟum. ks, ps eṉṟa mūccoliyillāp puṇarmeykaḷ iraṇṭum. uḷḷaṉa. ivviru moḻikkum potuvāka b,d,g,z eṉṉum nāleṭuppolikaḷ uḷa. eḻuttup puṇarcciyāl ēṟpaṭum jakara voliyum (J) kirēkkattiṟkuṇṭu. camaṟkirutattilō valliṉa volikaḷ aintiṟkum, kaṭuppoli eṭuppoliyākiya iruvaṭivilum, mūccoliyaic cērttum, ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil irupatu meykaḷaip piṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟuḷ, mūccoli cērnta pattum uṇmaiyil puṇar meykaḷē (consonantal diphthongs) kṣa (k+ṣa), jña mutaliya puṇar meykaḷum kūṭṭu meykaḷum ērāḷam.
vēta āriyark keḻuttiṉmai
vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntapōtu, avarkku eḻuttu millai; ilakkiyamumillai. vētattil mēṉāṭṭup poruḷ kaḷaip paṟṟiya kuṟippiruppiṉum. aḵtu eḻutappaṭṭatu vaṭa intiyāvilēyē mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilellām ekara okarak kuṟilum neṭilum uḷḷaṉa. kīḻaiyāriyattilō vaṭa tiraviṭattiṟ pōṉṟē ekara okarak kuṟilillai. āriyavētam mutalil oṉṟāyiruntu. piṉpu mūṉṟāki, iṟutiyil nāṉkā yiṟṟu. camaṟkirutam tōṉṟum varaiyum kīḻaiyāriyattiṟku eḻuttillai. āriya vētam nīṇṭakālamāka āciriyaṉāl vāyppāṭa māka ōtappeṟṟu māṇavarāl cevi vāyilākavē kaṟkappaṭṭu vantataṉāl, ataṟku āriyattil curuti yeṉṟum, tamiḻil eḻutākkiḷavi eṉṟum peyar eḻuntaṉa.
cru = kēḷ, cruti = kēḷvi, cru eṉpatu ceviyuṟu eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripēyeṉa vaṟika. iṅṅaṉam pala vaṭa coṟkaḷ tūya āriyamāyiṉum, tamiḻ vērkaḷiṉiṉṟē tirintavaiyē.
tamiḻ muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi yeḻunta āriya neṭuṅkaṇakku
vēta āriyar ki.mu. 1500 pōl teṉṉāṭu vantu tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē camaṟkirutam tōṉṟiṟṟu. aṉṟu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷum teṉ coṟkaḷum kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭatuṭaṉ. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkaip piṉpaṟṟi āriya neṭuṅkaṇakkum amaikkappaṭṭatu.
	camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakku tamiḻ muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ
(1) muṟai
mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟiṟkēṉum neṭuṅkaṇakkil muṟaiyillai. uyirum meyyum vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalantu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻilō, uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum, uyiruḷ kuṟil muṉṉum neṭil piṉṉum, kuṟiluḷ akaram muṉṉum ikaram iṭaiyum ukaram piṉṉum, ataṉpiṉ ekaramum iṟutiyil okaramum, ēkārattaiyaṭuttu aikāramum ōkārattaiyaṭuttu aukāramum;
meyyuḷ iṉavaliyum meliyumāka ivviraṇṭāy valliṉam muṉṉum melliṉam piṉṉum, iṭaiyiṉam avaṟṟiṉ piṉ ṉum, pintiyeḻunta nāleḻuttum iṟutiyilum; valliṉam ka ca ṭa ta pa eṉṟa muṟaiyilum, iṭaiyiṉam ya ra la va eṉṟa muṟai yilum.
kakarattiṟkiṉamāṉa nuṇṇoli (āytam) kakarattaiyaṭuttu muṉṉum, aṭittoṇṭai mutal itaḻ varaippaṭṭa eḻuttup piṟappiyal muṟai paṟṟi oḻuṅkāka vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulakil mutaṉ mutal im muṟaiyil eḻuttukaḷ vaikkappaṭṭa neṭuṅ kaṇakkuṭaiya moḻi tamiḻē, tamiḻil eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉi raṇṭaṉuḷ muṟai eṉpatum oṉṟu.
2. puṇarcci
eḻuttup puṇarcciyum mēlaiyāriya moḻikaṭkillai. itaṉāl, uyir meyyeḻuttumillai.
3. oppu
tamiḻāytattai orupuṭai yotta vaṭamoḻi vicarkkam, uyirukkum meykkum iṭaiyil vaikkappaṭṭiruppatu ūṉṟi nōkkattakkatu.
4. toṉmai
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku ki.mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumarik kaṇṭattil tōṉṟiyatu, vēta āriyar intiyāviṟku vanta kālam ki.mu. 2000-1500.
5. olinūṟpulamai
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil taṉiyeḻut tolikaḷē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. camaṟkiruta neṭuṅkaṇakkil. acaikaḷum puṇar meykaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; uyir meykaḷ uyirkaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ru lu mutaliya eḻuttukaḷ kuṟṟiyalukaram cērnta rakara lakara meykaḷ; am, a : eṉpavai akarattoṭu kūṭiya mūkkoli vicarkkaṅkaḷ; mūkkoli cērnta valliṉam puṇarmeykaḷ.
6. varuṭolikaḷ (Linguals or cerebrals)
ṭa,ṇa eṉṉum varuṭolikaḷ āriyattiṟkuriyavaiyalla. tamiḻuk kum tiraviṭattiṟkum tiraviṭa aṭippaṭaiyaik koṇṭa vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaṭkumē yuriyavai.
camaṟkiruta vari vaṭivu vakai
camaṟkiruta vari vaṭivu. kirantam, tēvanākari eṉa iru vakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muntiyatu kirantam, vēta āriyar tamiḻoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉ, tamiḻ varivaṭivaip piṉpaṟṟic camaṟkirutattiṟku amaittuk koṇṭatē kirantam. kirantam ēṟpaṭṭa piṉpum, vētam nīṇṭa kālam eḻutappaṭāmalē yiruntu vantatu. itai,
āṟṟa laḻiyumeṉ ṟantaṇarkaḷ nāṉmaṟaiyaip
pōṟṟiyurait tēṭṭiṉ puṟatteḻutār - ēṭṭeḻuti
vallunarum vallārum vaḷḷuvaṉār muppālaic
colliṭiṉum āṟṟalcōr viṉṟu.
eṉṉum tiruvaḷḷuvamālaic ceyyuḷ uṇarttum.
teṉṉāṭṭuk kirantattiṟkuppiṉ vaṭanāṭṭileḻunta vaṭa moḻi varivakai tēvanākari nakarattil allatu nākarikamākat tōṉṟiyateṉpatu paṟṟiyum, tēva moḻikkuriya teṉpatu paṟṟi yum, tēvanākari eṉappaṭṭatu. ataippaṟṟi māṉiyar ulliyamcu eṉpavar tam camaṟkiruta akara mutaliyiṉ muṉṉuraiyil 28ām pakka aṭik kuṟippil piṉ varumāṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
camaṟkirutak kalveṭṭukaḷuḷ mikap paḻamaiyāṉatāka aṟiyap paṭṭatu kattiyavāril cuṉākaril oru pāṟai mēluḷatu. aḵtu uruttira tāmaṉ kalveṭṭeṉṉum peyar koṇṭatu. atu ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatu. atu nākariyil illai; paḻaiya kalveṭṭeḻuttukaḷil uḷḷatu. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 400 ām āṇṭiṉatāṉa pavar kaiyeḻuttuppaṭi nākari nōkkiya peru muṉṉēṟṟattaik kāṭṭa, ki.pi. cumār 750 ām āṇṭiṉatāṉa tantiturukkaṉ kalveṭṭu iṟṟai vaḻakkiluḷḷa nākariyai mikavum otta oru niṟaivāṉa kuṟittokutiyaik kāṭṭuvatā yuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, uṇmaiyāṉa taṟkāla nākariyil uḷḷa mutaṟ kaiyeḻuttuppaṭi, ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭataṉ ṟeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
teṉṉāṭṭuk kirantamum vaṭanāṭṭut tēvanākariyum mutaṟkaṇ ulakiyaṟ kalai nūlkaṭkē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa. iṉṟu maṟai nūlkaḷum avaṟṟil eḻutappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
āḻa ūṉṟi nōkkum naṭunilai āyvāḷarkku, kirantamum tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiṉaveṉṉum uṇmai, muṟaiyē, veḷippaṭaiyākavum kuṟippākavum tōṉṟāmaṟ pōkātu.
	camaṟkirutavākkam - coṟkaḷ
vēta āriyar nāvalantēyam allatu nāvalampoḻil eṉṉum intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu, iṅkirunta ilakkiyam tamiḻilak kiyamē. avar intiya nākarikam tamateṉak kāṭṭit tammaik kumukāya (camutāya) nilaiyil ucca nilaikku uyarttuvāṉ vēṇṭi, tamiḻnūlkaḷai moḻi peyarttu ōr ilakkiyattai amaittaṟkup pōtiya colvaḷam tam muṉṉōr moḻik kiṉmaiyāl āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷait tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam kaṭaṉ koṇṭa mainta ilakkiyac ceyaṟkaik kalavai moḻiyē camaṟkirutam. itaṟku, ēṟkeṉavē iruvēṟu nilaikaḷil nērallā vaḻiyiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭirunta tamiḻccoṟkaḷum tuṇai ceytaṉa. itaṉāl, kīḻaiyāriyam mūvēṟu kālattil tamiḻc coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ koṇṭamai aṟiyappaṭum.
avaṟṟuḷ :
mutaṟkālam mēlaiyāriyak kālamākum. ak kālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavai mutaṟ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the First Period) eṉat takum.
iraṇṭāṅ kālam vēta āriyak kālamākum. akkālattil kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavai iraṇṭāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Second Period) eṉat takum.
mūṉṟāṅ kālam camaṟkirutak kālamākum. akkālattiṟ kaṭaṉ koḷḷappaṭṭavai mūṉṟāṅ kālat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (Tamil of the Third Period) eṉattakum.
1. mēlaiyāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
e-ṭu:
tiyūttāṉiyam
amma - amma. āṉ (= aṅku) yon. iru are. irum(pu) - iron. īṉ - yean, ean; uraṟu - roar, karai - cry, kāṇ - con, cun, can; kuraṅku (= vaḷai) - crank, kuruḷ - curl koṭukku crook, cappu - sup, sip; turuva through, nāku - nag paṭṭu (aṭi) - beat, pullam - bull, metu - smooth, muṉṉu - mun (think) vēṇṭu - want.
ilattīṉam
amar - amo (love), amor; uruḷai - uruṇṭa - rota (wheel), urai - or, kummal - cumulus. camam - similis. cēr - scr. tā - do. nāvāy - navis. paṭaku - barca. purai - par (equal), maṉ - man (remain) mutir - maturus. mel - mellow (soft), val - val (strong), vari (vaḷai) - vers (turn).
kirēkkam
aracaṉ - arthos, arkhon, kuru (niṟam) - chroma, kōṇam - gona. cāyai (niḻal) - skia. taccaṉ - tekton, tiruppu - trepos, turappaṇam - trapanon, tolai - telos. narampu - neuron. nīram - neros (wet). pala - polus, pari - pcri (round), paṟaḻ - proles, pāḻi (nakaram) - polis, purai - poros, mattikai - mastix, mastigos, māl - meals (black) viḻai - philos.
potu
pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēlaiyāriyattil pala moḻikaṭku allatu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaṭkup potuvākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
a. (cēymaic cuṭṭu) - E. a (that), Du. a (dat), Ger. a (das).
i. (aṇmaic cuṭṭu) - E.i (this) OE. (hit) Goth (hita), L. (hic).
ī. (aṇmaic cuṭṭu) - E. (these).
itōḷ (ivviṭam) - E. hither. OE. hider, L. citra.
(iy) - iyal - L. i,e; Gk. i ei; Goth. i. Slav. i, Lith. ei.
iyalutal = iyaṅkutal, naṭattal, cellutal. uy(iy) - iya - iyaṅku. iya - iyavu = celavu. vaḻi, iya - iyavuḷ = vaḻi. uytal = cellutal. uyttal = celuttutal.
ēr (kalappai) nu nayaca., ṭu. yaca.
oṉ - oṉṉu, oṉṟu E. one. Du. een, Ger. ein, L. unus, Gk. oinos.
kotti = pūṉai (kaṉṉaṭac col). pakaliṟ kuruṭu pōliruppatu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. L. catta; E.cat.
cālai - Ger. saal, It. sala, salone, F. salon, E. saloon, E. hall Du. and Dan. hal.
pati - patam - pātam. nilattiṟ pativatu patam allatu pātam. pātam - pātai = vaḻi. patam = vaḻi. aṭittaṭam toṭarntu pativataṉāl oru vaḻi amaiyum. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippākam atu. kāl, pātam, kīḻiṭam mutaliya pala poruḷ kaḷai yuṇarttum. pātam eṉpatu nilattiṟ patiyakkūṭiya paranta aṭippakuti. pātam vaitta viḷakku eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. aṭimaram paruttatu. marattaṭiyil uṭkārntāṉ. mēṭaik kaṭiyiṟ pār. aṭināḷiliruntu collivarukiṉṟēṉ, eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷiṉ pātam eṉṉum col poruntāmai kāṇka.
patam, pātam - Gk. podos. L. pedis. OE. fot (E. foot), Ger. fuss.
pātai - E. path, OE. paeth, Dut. pad, Ger. pfad.
bghW - OE., OHG. ber., Goth. bair, E. bear, L. fer, Gk. pher.
pōṭu - E.put, pot. pyt; Dan. putte. L. pon.
pōkaviṭu - pōkku - pōṭu.
iṅkuk kāṭṭiya coṟkaḷuḷ, kīḻaiyāriyattil illātaṉavu muḷa. avai mēlaiyāriyattiṉiṉṟu pirintu varumpōtu kīḻaiyāriyattāl iḻakkap paṭṭaṉavākum.
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ mēlaiyāriyattiṉum kīḻaiyāriyattil tirintiruppatu. piṉṉataṉ piṉmaiyaik kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
ammā - O.G. amma - Skt. amba.
kāṇ - OE. con - cna (metathesis) - E.know, OHG. cna, L. and Gk. gno - Skt. Jna (Ph.)
muḻuku - L. merg, Skt. majj.
irum(pu) - O.E. iren. O.G. Mod Ger. eise, Goth. eisarn. L. aer. aes; Skt. ayas.
val - L. val Skt. bal. 
cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ kīḻaiyāriyattiṉum mēlaiyāriyattil tirintiruppatu. vaṭatiraviṭam vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu paṭippaṭi yāyt tirintu airōppāvil muḻuvāriyamāy māṟiyataiyum. kīḻaiyāriyam intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉ vaṭaintiya vaṭivattaic cila coṟkaḷil mēṟkoṇṭataiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:
umpar - va.ti. ūppar. Teut. upper, over; L. super, Gk. huper. Skt. upari.
āyiram. ayir - (ayiram) - aciram). North Indian. hazar, Persian. hazar Gk. kesloi; kilioi; Skt. sahasra (sa+hasra).
cila tamiḻccoṟkaḷ tiyūttāṉiyattiṉum ilattīṉil tirintiruppatu. vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa tiraviṭam airōppāviṉ vaṭamēl mūlaiyai muṭṭit tirumpiyataiyum, tirumpukālil kiḻakku nōkkit tirintataiyum kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
turuva - Teut. through, L. trans.
tuḷ - turu - turuvu - turuva (vi.e.).
turuttu - Teut. thrust, L. trud (o) (intrude, protrude, obtrude etc).
2. vētavāriyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ
vētavāriyar vaṭa intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapōtu (ki.mu.2000 - 1500), aṅku vāḻntavaruṭ perumpālār tiraviṭarē. peluc cittāṉattilum vaṅkāḷattilum iṉṟum tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkuvataiyum, kāḷik kōṭṭam (Calcutta) eṉṉum peyar tūya tamiḻc collāyiruppataiyum, ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
āriya vētam vaṭatiraviṭam vaḻaṅkum nāṭṭil iyaṟṟap peṟṟamaiyāl, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ atiṟ pukuntuviṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: akavu. avai - cavai, ilakku, uttaram, uru - uruvu - uruvam, ulaku, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, kaṭukam, kālam, karuḷ (karumai), kumaraṉ - kumari, takkaṇam, taṇṭam, tūtu. naṭam, pakkam, maṇṭalam, mantiram, muttu - muttam, vaṭṭam.
virivañci ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkē iṅku mūlam viḷakkappeṟum. 
amalutal = neruṅkutal. amartal = neruṅkutal. amaital = neruṅkutal. niṟaital. poruntutal,cērtal, kūṭutal, tiraḷutal, amai = tiraṇṭa keṭṭi mūṅkil.
amai - avai = kūṭṭam. ma.va. pōli.
o.nō. ammai - avvai. kumi - kuvi.
avai - cavai. uyir mutaṟ coṟkaḷ collākkattil moḻi mutal meykaḷ ēṟappeṟum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cakaram.
e-ṭu: uruḷ - curuḷ, uvaṇam - cuvaṇam, ēṇ - cēṇ, ēmam - cēmam, capā (Sabha) eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyiṟ porutta māṉa vērillai.
uttaram = uyaram. uyaramāṉa vaṭaticai. takkaṇam = tāḻvu. tāḻvāṉa teṉticai. toṇṭai = tāḻnta kural. kuṭamalaiyāl uyarntiruppatu mēṟṟicaiyeṉṟum. kuṇakaṭalāl tāḻntiruppatu kīḻtticai yeṉṟam vaḻaṅki varutal kāṇka.
uruttal = tōṉṟutal. uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam. vaṭivuṭaip poruḷ. uru - uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumai vaṭivam. ataik kāṭṭum iṭaiccol. x.neh.: aḷavu - aḷapu. uruvu - uruvam. tamiḻilakkaṇam āriya varukaikku eṇṇāyiramāṇṭu muṟpaṭṭatu.
ulavutal = vaḷaital, cuṟṟutal. ulā = nakaraic cuṟṟi varum pavaṉi. ulakkai = uruṇṭa perumpiṭi. ulamvā - ulamā. ulamarutal = cuḻalutal. ulavu - ulaku = uruṇṭaiyāyiruppatu allatu cuṟṟi varuvatu. x.neh.: koṭkutal = cuṟṟi varutal. koḷ - kōḷ. ulaku - ulakam. lōk eṉṉam vaṭacol. pārttatu allatu pārkkappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. atu nōkku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripām.
iṉi, ulakam vaṭṭamāyirukkum ñālam (pūmi) eṉṟumām. x.neh.: kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam (kuṟun. 300).
ukaram uyarattaik kuṟikkum vērccol. e-ṭu: ukkam = talai. uvvi = talai. utti = oruvakait talaiyaṇi ucci, umpar, uvaṇ, uyar.
uvaṇ = mēliṭam. uvaṇai = tēvarulakam. uvaṇam = uyarcci, paruntu, kaluḻaṉ, kaḻuku. paruntu uyarap paṟakkum paṟavaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. uyaravuyarap paṟantālum ūrkkuruvi paruntākumā? eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. uvaṇam - cuvaṇam = paruntu, kaluḻaṉ. kaḻuku. uvaṇam (cuvaṇam) mullai nilattup paṟavaiyātalāl, an nilatteyvamākiya māyōṉukku ūrtiyākak kuṟikkappeṟṟatu.
cuvaṇam eṉṉum collai vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cuparṇa eṉṟu tirittup piṉpu cu + parṇa eṉṟu pirittu, nalla ilai, aḻakiya ilaiyuḷḷatu. aḻakiya ilai pōṉṟa ciṟakaiyuṭaiyatu. eṉṟu poruntāp puḷukalākak karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
kummutal = tiraḷutal. kum - kumar - kumaraṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ kumari = tiraṇṭavaḷ. iḷaiñai. o.nō. L virginem, E. virgin from virgo to swell viṭaittal = paruttal. vilaṅkiṉat tilum paṟavaiyiṉattilum tiraṇṭu iḷaimaiyā yiruppavaṟṟai viṭaiyeṉpatu marapu. āṭu, māṭu, kutirai, māṉ, pūṉai, kōḻi, pāmpu mutaliyavaṟṟuḷ, tiraṇṭu iḷamaiyāyiruppavaṟṟai viṭaiyeṉpatu iruvakai vaḻakkilumuṇmai kaṇṭukoḷka. viṭai - viṭalai = iḷaṅkāḷai, maṟavaṉ (vīraṉ), iḷaiñaṉ, 16 mutal 30 āṇṭu varaippaṭṭavaṉ. tiṇṇiyōṉ. iḷanīrai viṭalaiyeṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku. uyiriṉaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum āṇ paruttiruppatāl, viṭaiyeṉpatu iḷa vāṇaiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. kumar eṉṉum col uyar tiṇaiyil irupāṟ potuvām. kumari eṉpatu, iḷamai paṟṟic cila marappeyarkkum, putumai (mutaṉmai) kuṟittuc cila toḻiṟ peyarkkum, aḻiyāmai kuṟittuc cila iṭappeyar poruṭ peyarkkum aṭaimoḻiyām.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟkaḷai, vaṭavar kumāraṉ kumāri eṉa nīṭṭi, muṟaiyē tamiḻp poruḷoṭu makaṉ, makaḷ eṉṉum poruḷilum vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉar.
muḷ - muṇṭu - muṇṭai = muṭṭai. muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭai.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭiyiṭutal = kālai maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam. vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaḷaittal.
maṇṭalam eṉpatu irukkuvētap perumpiriviṉ peyar. tamiḻc ceyyuḷil ellāvaṭikaḷum aḷavottuvarum yāppu maṇṭilam eṉappaṭum.
ottā ḻicaiyum maṇṭila yāppum
kuṭṭamum nēraṭik koṭṭiṉa eṉpa  (cey. 114)
maṇṭilam kuṭṭam eṉṟivai yiraṇṭum
centūk kiyala eṉmaṉār pulavar  (cey. 116)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ. eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṉum vaḻinūṉ marapuraikaḷ. maṇṭilam eṉṉuñ col mutaṉūṟ kuṟiyīṭeṉpatait terivikkum.
maṇṭalam, maṇṭilam eṉṉum irucollum vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷkaḷaiyum, tōṟṟattaiyum celavaiyum kālattaiyum iṭattaiyum toṉṟutoṭṭu iruvakai vaḻakkilum kuṟittu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṟpatu allatu nāṟpattaintu nāṭ koṇ ṭatu maruttuva nūl kūṟum kāla maṇṭalam. toṇṭai maṇṭa lam. koṅku maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa paḻaimaiyāṉa iṭa maṇṭa lam. maṇṭilat tarumaiyum eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (977).
taḷ - tāḷ = marañ ceṭi koṭi payir pulliṉ taṭitta aṭi; ilai pūviṉ aṭi tāḷai oṭṭiya payir pullilai; tāḷ pōṉṟa kāl, viḷakkuttaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa.
taḷ - taṇṭu = ceṭikoṭikaḷiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi (e-ṭu: kīrait taṇṭu).
taṇṭu pōṉṟa poruḷ (e-ṭu: viḷakkuttaṇṭu).

x.neh.: = club = stick association. staff = stick, body of officers.

taḷ - taṭṭu, taṭṭai = cōḷap payir pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ tiraṇṭa aṭi.

tāṅkum pallakku.  taṇṭi - taṇṭiyam = tāṅkum kaṭṭai. taṇṭi - taṇṭikai = pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiyal = taṇṭikai.

niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa  (1434)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya nūṟpāvaiyum.
eṇṇiya veṇṇiyāṅ keytupa veṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ  (kuṟaḷ 666)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷaiyum, tirumūlar tirumantirattaiyum mantiram collic cavittuviṭṭāṉ. mantirattiṉāl vāyaik kaṭṭiviṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷaiyum; mantiri, mantiram (cūḻviṉai) eṉṉum araciyaṟ coṟkaḷaiyum, kūrntu nōkkuka.
vāymoḻi eṉpatu mantirattiṉ maṟupeyar, nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalil nammāḻvār aruḷicceyta mutalāyiram tiruvāy moḻiyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
ākavē, mantiram eṉṉum col tamiḻil araciyal, mataviyal, ilakkaṇam ākiya muttuṟaikkuriyatākum. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya varacukaḷ varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāt toṉmai vāyntavai. paṇṭai nāḷiṟ kiḻakkattiya nāṭukaṭkut tamiḻnāṭṭoṭu araciyal toṭarpum vaṇikat toṭarpum iruntatiṉāl. mantiri eṉṉum col malēyā cīṉam mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum paraviyiruk kiṉṟatu. Mandarin eṉṉum āṅkilac col pōrttukīciya vāyilāyp piṟkālattiṟ pukuntatākum. ataṟkumuṉ mēlai yāriyattil ic col illai.
camaṟkiruta iru paṭaikaḷ
1. aṭippaṭai - vēta āriyam
2. mēṟpaṭai 	1. vaṭa tiraviṭam (pirākirutam)
			2. teṉ tiraviṭam 
			   (tamiḻoḻinta pañca tiraviṭam)
			3. tamiḻ.
pirākirutam eṉpatu. vētak kālattil vaṭa intiyāvil vaḻaṅ kiya vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷ. pirākirutam = muntic ceyyappaṭṭatu. camaṟkirutam eṉpatu, vētamoḻi yilakkaṇa vamaitiyaip perumpālum taḻuvi, am moḻiyoṭu pirākirutattaiyum tiraviṭattaiyum (teṉ tiraviṭam) tamiḻaiyum kalantu ceyta araic ceyaṟkaiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭaimoḻiyākum. camaṟ kirutam = naṉṟāyc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
pirākirutam eṉṉum col pākatam eṉṟum, camaṟkirutam eṉṉum col caṅkatam, caṉukkirakam allatu cāṉukkirakam eṉṟum, tiriyum.
pirākirutam camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyateṉpatu ataṉ peyarālum moḻiyālum teḷivāyt teriyiṉum. camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu pirākirutam tirintatākap pētaimai mikka paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷaiyum ārāycciyillāta ayalāraiyum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar mayakki varu kiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam). camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ tirintum tiriyāmalum pākatattil vaḻaṅkuvatē.
e-ṭu:tiriyātavai 	tirintavai
	caṣṭi (sashti)		caṭṭi (satihi)
	tarma (dharma)		tamma (dhamma)
tirintu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa. tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭāntam (truṣ ṭānta), cōttam (tōtra) eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷ pōṉ ṟavaiyē. avai yāvum aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷalla. pirākirutat tiṟkum aṭippaṭai tamiḻē āyiṉum. atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟāta vāṟu attuṇai vaṭacoṟkaḷ atiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa. vaṭacoṟ kalappu tamiḻil iruppataiviṭat tiraviṭattil mikutiyākavum, tiraviṭattil iruppataiviṭap pākatattil mikutiyākavum, irukkum. vaṭakkē cellac cella vaṭacoṟ kalappu mikum eṉa aṟika. vēta āriya moḻi vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉataṉāl, atu paḻaṅ kuṭi moḻikaḷiṟ kalantu pulluruviyum uṇṇiyum pēṉum pōl mēluṇṇiyāy (Parasite) iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āriyac col vērūṉṟiyavuṭaṉ ataṟku nērāṉa teṉcol vaḻak kaṟṟup pōm. vaḻakkaṟṟa col ilakkiyattil iṭam peṟā viṭiṉ maṟaintu mīḷā nilaiyaṭaiyum.
pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatiraviṭattiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ perumpālum īṟu tirintē vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu: tamiḻ 	pirākirutam 	tamiḻ 	   pirākirutam
	accaṉ 	ajja 		kaṭṭai	kaṭṭa
	attai 	attā 	kampam 	kampa
	ēḻakam 	ēḷaka 		nēyam 	nēyam
	aiyaṉ 		ayya 		vaṭṭam 	vaṭṭa

camaṟkirutat tamiḻc coṟkaḷ
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷellām, kīḻppaṭaiyum mēṟpaṭaiyum paṟṟi,
1. paḻañcol
2. putuccol eṉṟum;
moḻi vakai paṟṟi.
1. āriyac col
2. tamiḻc col eṉṟum;
vērp poruṭ karaṇiyam uṇmaiyum iṉmaiyum paṟṟi.
1. karaṇiyakkuṟi
2. iṭukuṟi eṉṟum.
ivviru pālāyp pakukkappaṭum.
paḻañcol eṉpaṉa vēta āriyam. iṭukuṟikaḷ āṉanta pairavi kaliyāṇi, caṅkarāparaṇam, taṉṉiyāci, nāta nāmak kiriyai, mutaliya paṇṇup peyarkaḷ pōṉṟavai.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu mūvakaiyāy vakukkap peṟum.
(1) muḻuccol : e-ṭu: : akavu, tūtu.
(2) vērccol : e-ṭu: paṇ - pāṇi (kai), maṉ - manu.

al - aṉ - aṉu = kūṭa.

ā eṉpatu ōr etirmaṟaiyiṭaiccol. tā = koṭu.

avamāṉam. aṉumāṉam, upamāṉam, caṉmāṉam, nirmāṇam, piramāṇam, parimāṇam mutaliya pala coṟkaḷ māṉam eṉṉum tamiḻc collai varumoḻiyākayum īṟākavuṅ koṇṭavai.
tamiḻccol tirippu muṟaikaḷ mūvakait tiripu
(1) tōṉṟal
pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ. mutalayalil rakaramiṭaic cerukiyum vakaramiṭaic cerukiyum vaṭacollākkappaṭṭuḷa.
e-ṭu : ra :	tamiḻ - tramiṭa - traviṭa.
		tiṭam - truṭa.
		tūṇi - trōṇa, tōṇi - trōṇa.
		paṭi - prati, paṭimai - pratimā.
		patikam - pratīka, pavaḷam - pravāḷa.
		puṭṭam - pruṣṭa, puṭavi - prutvī.

ivaṟṟait talaikīḻākat tirippatu vaṭanūlār iyalpu.
(6) valitturaittal
irumoḻikaḷ kuṟukiya kālattil aṭuttu vaḻaṅkiṉum, oṉṟi ṉiṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟu oru cila coṟkaḷaiyēṉum koḷḷā tirātu. vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻiyoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu mūvāyiram āṇṭiṟku mēlāyiṉum, atiṟ kalantuḷḷa āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷum oṉṟaiyēṉum teṉ colleṉa oppuk koḷḷātu vaṭa collēyeṉa valippatu. iṉṟum vaṭa moḻiyāḷar iyalpāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu. vaṭa moḻiyi luḷḷa teṉ coṟkaḷ aintiliraṇṭu pakutiyākum.
(7) tēvamoḻiyeṉal
vēta āriyar, paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ pētaimaiyaiyum matappittaiyum aḷaviṟantu payaṉpaṭutti, tammai nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum, tam muṉṉōr moḻiyāṉa vēta āriyattaiyum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa camaṟkirutattaiyum tēva moḻiyeṉṟum collit tamiḻar atai muṟṟum nampumāṟu ceytu viṭṭataṉāl, tamiḻil aḷaviṟanta vaṭacoṟkaḷ vēṇ ṭātu kalakkavum. ataṉāl atu taṉ vaḷaṅkuṉṟit tūymai yiḻakkavum, vaḻipāṭṭiṟkut takātateṉṟu taḷḷappaṭavum, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum tamiḻar tammait tāḻttik koḷḷa vum, nērntateṉa aṟika.  (teṉmoḻi ceppiṭempar 1963.)
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ
(1) cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ
tiraviṭattiṟ pōṉṟē āriyattilum, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ a, i, u eṉṉum muttamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷaiyē mūlamākak koṇṭaṉa.
tamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyavaiyeṉṟum, āriyattiṉ mūla moḻiyoṭu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟum, kālṭuvelār tēṟṟamākak kūṟiyiruttalaik kāṇka.
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ, tiricoṟkaḷātalāl, ōreḻuttuc collā yirātu pala veḻuttuc collākavē yirukkum.
e-ṭu:	ata = atu, appaṭi, aṅkē.
		iha = iṅkē, ivviṭattil, ivvulakil.
		cila cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ iṭamāṟic cuṭṭum.
e-ṭu:	atra - iṅkē, atuṉā = ippoḻutu.
		cila cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ cēymai yaṇmai yiraṇṭaiyum cuṭṭum.
e-ṭu:	atta (atas) = ataṉāl, itaṉāl.
		ikarac cuṭṭu ēkāra mākavum tiriyum.
e-ṭu:	ētat (etad) = itu, iṅkuḷḷatu, iṅku.
		ēvam = ippaṭi.

ic coṟkaṭ kellām vērum mūlamum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻ āriyattiṟku mūlam eṉpatai nāṭṭaṟkut tā eṉṉum col oṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām. tā-ca. tā, tat (dad), ilat. tō, kirēk. titō.
ic col tamiḻil toṉṟu toṭṭup peruvaḻakkāyiruntu varuvatuṭaṉ,
ītā koṭuveṉak kiḷakkum mūṉṟum
iraviṉ kiḷavi ākiṭa ṉuṭaiya.  (tol. ec. 48)
avaṟṟuḷ,
īyeṉ kiḷavi iḻintōṉ kūṟṟē.  (mēṟpaṭi. 49)
tāeṉ kiḷavi oppōṉ kūṟṟē.  (mēṟpaṭi. 50)
koṭuveṉ kiḷavi uyarntōṉ kūṟṟē  (mēṟpaṭi. 51)
tarucol varucol āyiru kiḷaviyum
taṉmai muṉṉilai āyī riṭatta.  (mēṟpaṭi. kiḷavi. 29)
eṉṉum varampukaṭ kuṭpaṭṭa ciṟappup poruḷatāyum, tā eṉṉum tantai peyarāyum, uḷḷatu. kaṉṉaṭattilum malai yāḷattilum pōṉṟē āriyattilum potup poruḷil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, avvāriya moḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu ic collaik kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu, teritaru tēṟṟamām.
orē teṉcol mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tirinta tiricoṟkaḷ, camaṟkirutattil vevvēṟu mutaleḻuttup peṟṟu vevvēṟu mūlattiṉa pōl tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: cāy (mutaṉilai). cāytal = kōṇutal, vaḷaital, viḻutal, paṭuttal, iṟattal.
cāyuṅ kālam = poḻutu cāyum eṟpāṭu. poḻutu cāya vantāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
cāyuṅ kālam - cāyaṅ kālam - cāyṅ kālam (koccait tiripu.)
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cāyaṅ kālam eṉpataic cāyam + kālam eṉat tavaṟākap pirittu, cāyam eṉpatai eṟpāṭṭiṉ peyarākak koṇṭu, atai vaṭamoḻiyil sāyam eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
cāy - cāyai = cāyum niḻal. niḻal cāykiṟatu eṉpatu vaḻakku. cāyai - ca. cāya (cha#ya#). niḻal vaṭivattaiyum niṟattaiyum kuṟikku mātalāl, cāyal, cāyam mutaliya coṟkaḷum tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa.
cāy - cāyi = paṭuttavaṉ, paḷḷi koṇṭāṉ. cēṣa cāyi = aravaṇait tuyiṉṟōṉ. talai cāyttal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. cāy - cayaṉam = paṭukkai.
cāy - cā. viḻutalum paṭuttalum iṟattalaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. cā - cāvu - cavam. āḷ cāyntu viṭṭāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu, otta poruṭ karaṇiyamuḷḷa vēṟoru collaiyum paṭait tirukkiṉṟaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
e-ṭu: cuḷ - cur - curam = cuṭum pālai nilam.
curam - jvara = cuṭum kāyccal nōy.
cila teṉcol mutaṉilaikaḷiṉiṉṟu, ērāḷamāṉa putuc coṟ kaḷait tōṟṟuvittuc camaṟkirutattai vaḷampaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar.
e-ṭu: pū (mutaṉilai). pūttal = tōṉṟutal, uṇṭātal, iruttal.
pūttaliṟ pūvāmai naṉṟu  (nītineṟi. 6)
pūttiḻi matamalai  (kamparā. kumpakar. 315)
puku - poku - pokil - arumpu.
pokil - pōkil = arumpu.
puku - pō - pōttu = putitāy veṭikkum ciṟu kiḷai.
pū - pūtu - pūtam = tōṉṟiya aimpūtaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  (any of the five elements)
pū - bhu#. itiṉiṉṟu tāṉ puvaṉam, puvi, pūti, pūmi, pavam, pavaṉam, pavitram, paviṣyam, pāvam, pāvakam, pāvi, pāvaṉai, pāvikam, apāvam, aṉupavam, aṉupavi, aṉupūti, utpavam, campu, tatpavam, prapu, prapāvam, paripavam, sampavam, sampāvitam, sampāvaṉai, cuyampū mutaliya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ piṟakkum. tamiḻarkku eḷitāy viḷaṅkumāṟu ivai iṅku tamiḻeḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷ, tirintum muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟum urumāṟi vaṭacoṟkaḷāki yuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: āyiram - aram - ara - sakara.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷ mēlai yāriya vaḻiyākat tirintu, nampa muṭiyā aḷavu camaṟkirutattil muṟṟum vaṭivu māṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:	kāṇ - Teut. kun, ken, con, cun, can, know, L. gno, ḻumu. panṭi, ḷumava. tan!yaja. (jñā). kāṭci (aṟivu) - jñāṉa.
cila teṉcoṟkaḷaic camaṟkirutattil moḻi peyarttu amaittuḷḷaṉar. e-ṭu: vilaṅku - triyac, aṅkuṟṟai - tatrapavat.
camaṟkirutam ilakkiya moḻiyum ceyaṟkai moḻiyumātalāl, pala poruḷkaṭkum poruṭ pākupāṭukaṭkum viruppam pōl iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷaip paṭaittuḷḷaṉar.
e-ṭu: eḻu mukil (sapta mēkam) : camvarttam, āvarttam, puṭkalā varttam, caṅkārittam, turōṇam, kāḷamuki, nīlavarṇam eṉpaṉa.
caṅkarāparaṇam, nāta nāmak kiriyai mutaliya nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa paṇṇup peyarkaḷum iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷē.
kallāla mara nīḻaliṟ civaperumāṉiṭam pāṭaṅ kēṭṭa āriya muṉivar peyarākac collappaṭum caṉakar, caṉantaṉar, caṉātarar, caṉaṟ kumārar eṉpaṉavum ittakaiyaṉavē.
iṅṅaṉam, camaṟkirutattil aintiliru pakuti muḻut tamiḻc col; aintiliru pakuti tamiḻ vēriṉiṉṟu tirinta tiri col; aintiloru pakuti iṭukuṟic col.
perum pērāciriyar u.vē. cāminātaiyar, tāmvarainta kuṟuntokai yuraiyuḷ, nūlārāycci eṉṉun talaippiṉ kīḻ, 44 coṟkaḷai vaṭacollākak kāṭṭi, mutaliyaṉa eṉṉuñ collāl muṭittu, ivaṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟait tamiḻeṉavē koḷvārum uḷar. eṉṟu eḻuti yirukkiṉṟār.
avaṟṟuḷ, akil, amayam, amiḻtam, aracaṉ, avai, āriyar, ulakam, ēmam, kaṭikai, kalāvam, kāmam, kālam, kuṇaṉ, kuvaḷai, cakaṭam, cūli, cēmam, cēri, taṇṭu, tātu, tūtu, teyvam, nakar, nīlam, pakkam, paṇilam, paruvam, pavaḻam, maṇṭilam, maṇi, matam, mati, mālai, mukam, muttu, muracu, yāmam eṉṉum muppattēḻum teṉcollām.
avi - aviḻ = cōṟṟup parukkai, cōṟu. aviḻ - aviḻtu - amiḻtu - amitu - amutu = cōṟu, uṇavu. parup pamutu, kaṟiyamutu, tayiramutu, ilaiyamutu eṉpaṉa uṇṇuṅ kaṟivakai kaḷaik kuṟikkum kalveṭṭuc coṟkaḷ.
amutu ceytal = uṇṇutal. amutu paṭaittal = uṇavu parimāṟutal. tiruvamutu = teyvap paṭimaikkup paṭaikkappaṭum uṇavu.
amiḻtu - amiḻtam. amutu - amutam.
kaṭṭip poruḷāyiṉum, nīrp poruḷāyiṉum, uṇṇap paṭuvatellām amutamām. uṇaviṟkuc cuvaiyūṭṭum uppum amutam eṉappaṭṭatu.
amutu = 1. cōṟu. 2. uṇavu. vāṭā malarum nallamutum (ñāṉavā. pirakalā. 8) 3. pāl. (aka.ni.). 4. nīr. (piṅ.).
amutam = 1. cōṟu (piṅ.) 2. pāl. (piṅ.). 3. nīr. tulaṅkiya vamutam  (kallā. 5).
uṇṭavaṉ vāḻvāṉ; uṇṇātavaṉ cāvāṉ. cōṟum nīrum cāvait tavirppatāl iru marunteṉappaṭum.
tayir amutam eṉappaṭuvatāl veṇṇeyum amutamām.
tēvar makkaḷiṉum uyarntava rātalāl, avaruṇavu ciṟantatākak karutappaṭṭatu.
ēṉatu cuvaippiṉum nīkai toṭṭatu
vāṉō ramutam puraiyumāl emakkeṉa  (tol. kaṟ. 5).
amutam eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil amruta eṉṟu tiriyum. atai a+mrut eṉṟu pirittuc cāvait tavirppatu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu, ataṟ kēṟpat tēvarum acurarum kūṭit tiruppāṟ kaṭal kaṭaintu veṇṇe yeṭuttatākak katai kaṭṭi viṭṭaṉar. akkataiyiṟ pakuttaṟiviṟ kovvāta pala ceyti kaḷiruttal kāṇka.
mantiram mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiyatu tamiḻilēyē.
mantirap poruḷvayiṉ āakunavum  (tol. ecca. 53)
muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻu muṉivar tam tūya vuḷḷattiṉ eṇṇa valimai yāl āyntu kūṟum meym moḻi, niṟaimoḻi māntariṉ niṟaivēṟuṅ kūṟṟu.
mantiram vāymai paṟṟiyum vāyppatu paṟṟiyum vāymoḻi yeṉavum paṭum.
civaṉiyac cārpāṉa tirumantiramum māliyac cārpāṉa tiruvāy moḻiyum tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa.
tattuva maci
ōkanilaikkuriya tattuva maci eṉṉum coṟṟoṭarai, peruṅ kūṟṟiyam (makā vākkiyam) eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyāḷar paṟai yaṟaivar. atu muṟṟum teṉ coṟṟiri peṉpatai avar aṟiyār.
tat + tvam + asi = tattvamasi.
tāṉ, tām eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutaṟ kālattil orumaiyum paṉmaiyuṅ kuṟitta paṭarkkaic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷāka viruntu, akara vaṭi koṇṭa aimpāṟ paṭarkkaic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṟkālattil, taṟcuṭṭup peyarkaḷāka (Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiṉa.
tāṉ (avaṉ, avaḷ, atu.) - ca. tat = avaṉ, avaḷ, atu.
x.neh.: tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ - ca. srīmat.
OE. thæt, E. that.
vētap peyarkaḷ
vēta moḻiyilum camaṟkirutattilum nūṟṟuk kaṇakkum āyirak kaṇakkumāṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷ iruppatuṭaṉ, vētattiṉ peyarkaḷum teṉcol tiripākavē uḷḷaṉa.
viḻittal = kaṇ tiṟattal, pārttal, aṟital, viḻi = aṟivu, ōti (ñāṉam).
viḻak kaṇṭun tēṟār viḻiyilā māntar (tiruman.) viḻi L. vide, OS. wit, Goth, wit, ON. vit, Skt. - vit - vēta = aṟivu, aṟivu nūl, maṟai.
ceviyuṟutal = kēṭṭal. ceviyuṟu - cru - cruti = kēḷvi, eḻu tāk kiḷaviyāyk kēṭṭaṟiyap paṭṭa āriya maṟai.
vētap piriviṉ peyarāṉa maṇṭalam eṉpatum teṉcollē.
maṇṭutal = vaḷaital. maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭi yiṭutal = kālai vaḷaittu niṟṟal allatu iruttal. maṇṭu - maṇṭalam - vaṭṭam, nāṭṭu vaṭṭam, kāla vaṭṭam, vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ, nūṟ pirivu. maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam. eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu pārkka.
mūṉṟaṉ pakutiyum maṇṭilat tarumaiyum  (tol. akat. 41)
toṇṭai maṇṭalam, koṅku maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa toṉmai yāṉa nāṭṭup peyarkaḷ.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaṭṭamiṭutal, pāṭṭiṉ ellā aṭikaḷum aḷavot tiruttal.
maṇṭilam kuṭṭam eṉṟivai yiraṇṭum  (tol. cey. 116)
vaṭṭamiṭum allatu vaṭṭamāyirukkum, pāmpu, naccup pūcci mutaliya uyirikaḷum, narappicaik karuvikaḷum, maṇṭalam allatu maṇṭali eṉap peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
ūṉ, ūm eṉṉum muṉṉilaip peyarkaḷ, nūṉ, nūm eṉṟu nakara meym mutal peṟṟu, piṉṉar nīṉ, nīm eṉat tirintaṉa.
nūṉ, nūm eṉpaṉa, vaṭatiraviṭattuḷ oru vakaiyāṉa cūracēṉip pirākirutattil tūṉ, tūm eṉṟu tirintu, iṉṟa ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa intiyil tū, tum eṉṟa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
tū (nī) eṉṉum inti vaṭivamē, iyalpākavum kuṟukiyum mēlai āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
OE. OS. thu#, OHG. du#, ON. thu, Goth. thu, L. tu, E. thou.
ēṉ, ēm eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum nāṉ nām eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum iṭaiyē, yāṉ yām eṉpavai iruppatu pōṉṟē, ūṉ ūm eṉpavaṟ ṟiṟkum nūṉ nūm eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum iṭaiyē, yūṉ yūm eṉpaṉa vum iruntaṉa.
iv yakara aṭiyiṉiṉṟē āṅkila cākacaṉīyam yī (ye) allatu yū (you) eṉṉum muṉṉilaip paṉmaip peyarait tōṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
matippuṟavu paṟṟit tum eṉṉum paṉmaip peyarai intiyum, yū eṉṉum paṉmaip peyarai āṅkilamum, orumaikku vaḻaṅku vatu pōl, vēta āriyamum, tum eṉṉum paṉmaip peyarait tvam eṉṟu tirittu orumaikkum, yuvām yūyam eṉṉum yakara vaṭip peyarkaḷai muṟaiyē irumai paṉmaikkum, vaḻaṅ kiṟṟup pōlum.
ākavē, nūm eṉṉum muṉṉilaip paṉmaip peyariṉ tiripē, intiya āriyattiluḷḷa tvam eṉṉum muṉṉilai yorumaip peyar eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.
a
camaṟkiruta muṉṉilai yorumai nikaḻkāla viṉaiyīṟu si. a eṉṉum viṉai itaṉoṭu puṇarum pōtu īṟu keṭṭu asī eṉa niṟkum. ākavē, tat, tvam, asi eṉṉum muccol lum cērntu, nī atuvāyirukkiṉṟāy eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum tattvamasi eṉṉum kūṟṟiyam amaiyum.
tat eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyariṉiṉṟē tatva (tattuvam) eṉṉum collum piṟakkum.
tatva = atāyirukkum taṉmai, uṇmaiyāṉa taṉmai, uṇmai, meyp poruḷ.
vaṭacol eṉpataṟkum camaṟkirutac col eṉpataṟkum oru vēṟṟumai yuṇṭu. toṉṟu toṭṭu vaṭanāṭṭil taṉic ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkuñ collellām vaṭa collē. camaṟkirutac col eṉpatu, vēta āriyar vantapiṉ putitāka amaikkappaṭṭa āriyac col. āriyar varumuṉpē, vaṭa tiraviṭar (pirākirutar) pala putuc coṟkaḷaip puṉaintiruntaṉar. avaiyum vaṭacol. ākavē, vaṭacol eṉṉum peyar, āriyamallāta vaṭanāṭṭuc ciṟappuc coṟkum, camaṟkirutac coṟkum, potuvām. ellāc camaṟkirutac collum vaṭa collākum; āyiṉ, ellā vaṭacollum camaṟkiruta mākā.
āti eṉṉum col vaṭacollē; camaṟkirutamaṉṟu. atu kaḻaka ilakkiya vaḻakkil illai. āyiṉum, uṇṭātal eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa ātal viṉaiyiṉiṉṟu atait tirippar tamiḻaṉpar cilar.
ākkutal = paṭaittal. aṉaittulakum ākkuvāy kāppāy aḻippāy (tiruvāca. 1: 42). ivvāṭci avarkkut tuṇai ceyyalām.
camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam
tamiḻilakkaṇattaip piṉpaṟṟic camaṟkirutattil mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya ilakkaṇam aintiram. ataṉ kālam tōrā. ki.mu. 8-ām allatu 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ataṉpiṉ, ki.mu.4 ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu aṣṭāt yāyī eṉṉum pāṇi ṉīyam. iraṇṭum eḻuttum collum ākiya iru kūṟukaḷaiyē koṇṭaṉa.
eḻuttiṉ muṟai, piṟappu, uyirmeyt taṉi vaṭivam, māttirai, kiḷavi (patam), puṇarcci ākiyavaṟṟil, camaṟkiruta ilakkaṇam tamiḻaiyē taḻuviyuḷḷatu. eḻuttiṉ vari vaṭivum, kirantam nākari eṉṉum iru vakaiyilum perumpālum tamiḻai ottiruppatu, kūrntu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākum.
peyar viṉai iṭai eṉṉum tamiḻp pākupāṭṭaiyē, camaṟkirutac colli lakkaṇamum taḻuviyuḷḷatu. vēṟṟumaikaḷ ilattīṉil aintu; kirēkkattil āṟu; camaṟkirutattil tamiḻiṟ pōl eṭṭu. itaṉāl, vēṟṟumai yamaippil, camaṟkirutam tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
camaṟkiruta ilakkiyam
patiṉeṇ toṉmaṅkaḷum (purāṇaṅkaḷum) irāmāyaṇam pāratam ākiya maṟa vaṉappukkaḷum (itikācaṅkaḷum) tavira, icai, nāṭakam, kaṇiyam, maruttuvam mutaliya piṟa kalai yaṟiviyal ilakkiya mellām tamiḻ mutaṉūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi nūlkaḷām. camaṟkirutattil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭapiṉ, tamiḻ mutaṉūlka ḷellām aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa.
camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiya intiya āriya ilakkiyam vētam oṉṟē. vētattaip peṇṭirum cūttirarum ōtak kūṭāteṉpar. āyiṉ, peṇṭirum cūttirarum cilar vēta mantiraṅkaḷaiyē iyaṟṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar.
vētam iyaṟṟiya peṇṭir
pulōmaṉai 	makaḷ 		ṣaci
kāmai 		makaḷ 		cirattai
cakti 		makaḷ 		kōravi
ap pirīṉā 	makaḷ 		vākku
akattiyar 	maṉaivi	lōpā muttirai
vētam iyaṟṟiya paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ
aracar (cattiriyar) mantāttiri, ṣivi, vacumaṉāṉ, piratarttaṉaṉ, vivāmittiraṉ, matu cantaṉaṉ, riṣapaṉ, rēṇu, ampariṣaṉ, parataṉ, mētāniti, nāpakaṉ, irakukaṇaṉ, vakṣap piriyaṉ, purūravaṉ, vēṉaṉ, cutācaṉ, kiruta camataṉ, tēvāpi, cantāṉu mutaliyōr.
toḻilāḷar (cūttirar) : kavaṣaṉ, ailuṣaṉ mutaliyōr.
kalappuk kulattār : kakṣīvatu (aṅka nāṭṭu aracaṉiṉ puḻukkacci makaṉ) pōṉṟa palar.
ippeyarkaḷ varuṇa cintāmaṇiyil uḷḷavāṟē eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
āriyar karuttuppaṭi, vaṭa nāṭṭu makkaḷē pirāmaṇar, cattiriyar, vaiciyar, cūttirar eṉṉum nālvakaip pākupāṭṭiṉar; teṉ ṉāṭṭut tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum ākiya antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar aṉaivarum cūttirarē. āyiṉ, āṭci ceyyum aracaraic cattiriyar eṉṟum, poruḷ koṭukkum vaṇikarai vaiciyar eṉṟum, pukaḻvar. itu atikāramum poruḷum ceyyum vēlai. vēḷvi ceyvitta paṇṭai mūvēntaraiyum, cattiriyar eṉṟu pukaḻntē vēḷvi ceyvikkat tūṇṭiṉar.
vētattiṉ piṉ vēḷvi muṟaikaḷai viḷakkum pirāmaṇaṅkaḷum, āriyar tamiḻaroṭu toṭarpu koṇṭapiṉ upaniṭataṅkaḷum kulavoḻukka nūlkaḷākiya taruma cāttiraṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
vētam ceyyuḷ vaṭivāṉa mantiraṅkaḷaik koṇṭatu. mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiyatu irukku (ruk) oṉṟē. vēḷvi ceytaṟ kuriya mantiraṅkaḷai atiṉiṉṟu pirittut tokuttatu ecur (yajur). vēḷviyiṟ pāṭak kūṭiya mantiraṅkaḷaip pirittut tokuttatu cāmam (sāma). iṅṅaṉam oru vētam mūvētam (vētatrayī) āyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ āṟiloru pakuti irukku vēta mantiraṅ kaḷaik koṇṭatum, perumpālum cāvippu mantiraṅkaḷā lāṉatum, urainaṭai kalantatumāṉa, atarvaṉam (atarvaṉ) tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉṉarē nāṉ maṟai (catur vēta) eṉṉum vaḻakkeḻuntatu.
vētak kālattiṉ piṉ, vēta cāttiraṅkaḷiṉ poruḷaiyum payaṉaiyum ciṟappaiyum perumpālum urainaṭaiyil viḷak kum uraiyākat tōṉṟiyavai pirāmaṇam. kāṭuṟaivu (vāṉap piratta) nilaiyiṟ pirāmaṇaṉukkup payaṉpaṭum koṇ muṭipu nūlkaḷ āraṇiyakam (āraṇyaka). tuṟavu (canniyāsa) nilaiyiṟ pirāmaṇaṉukkup payaṉpaṭum meypporuḷ nūlkaḷ upaniṭatam (upa niṣat).
upa niṣat = uṭaṉ kīḻirukkai. upa = uṭaṉ, ni - kīḻ, sat (Sad) = iruttal. āciriyaṉ aṭi yarukiruntu avaṉ nuvaṟciyaik kēṭṭal eṉpatē viḷakkap poruḷ. paramporuḷaṟivāl aṟiyāmaiyai ōyn tirukkac ceykai eṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. naṉku aḻittaṟku sātaṉam eṉpar par. (P.S.) cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār.
vētat teyviyam palteyviyamē (Polytheism). palloru teyviyam (Honotheism) eṉṟu mākcu mullarum, oru teyviyam (Monotheism) eṉṟu mākaṭāṉelum (Mac Donell) kūṟiyatu tavaṟām. āriyar paramporuḷaṟivait tamiḻariṭamē peṟṟaṉar. palciṟu teyva vēḷvi matattiṟkum param poruṭ camayat tiṟkum maṭuviṟkum malai muṭikkum uḷḷa tolaivē.
sat eṉṉum collum teṉ col tiripē.
kuttu - kuntu, L. Sid, Sed, AS. Sitt, E. Sit, Goth. Sit. ON, Siti, OHG. Sizz, Skt. Sad.
ki.pi. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ, kōvilamaippu, paṭimai yamaippu, vaḻipaṭu muṟai, pūcakaṉ takuti, koṇ muṭipu mutaliya vaṟṟaik kūṟum toḻu maṟaikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, civa ṉiyattiṟkuriyavai tōṉṟiyam (ākamam) eṉṟum, māliyattiṟ kuriyavai tokuppiyam (samhitai) eṉṟum, kāḷiyattiṟku (cāk tattiṟku) uriyavai pāvakam (tantiram) eṉṟum peyar peṟum.

tēva moḻiyiṉ iyalpukaḷ
toṉmai, muṉmai, iyaṉmai, viyaṉmai, tāymai, tūymai, eṇmai, oṇmai, iḷamai, vaḷamai, ammai, cemmai, iṉimai, taṉimai, maṟaimai, iṟaimai eṉṉum patiṉāṟum tēva moḻikku irukka vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta paṇpu kaḷām. ippatiṉāṟum uṭaiyatu tamiḻ oṉṟē. vaṭa moḻikku ōriru paṇpukaḷē uḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻaip pōṟ piṟa moḻi kalavātu pēcat takka moḻi ulakil vēṟoṉṟu millai. āriya moḻikaḷ piṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷai vilakkiṉum, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷai vilakkavē muṭiyātu. ātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ uyir vāḻviṟkut tamiḻt tuṇai iṉṟiyamaiyāta tām. ākavē, tamiḻē tēva moḻi eṉṟu teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
camaṉ
camaṉ: ammutal = poruntutal, ottal am - cam - camam = oppu. camam - camaṉ = tulaikkōl. niṟukkumuṉ irupuṟamum ottu niṟkum nilai. camam - sama (va.).  (ti.ma.: 742)



caram
caram - sā
nīrpporuḷ oru tuḷaiyiṉiṉṟu nērāka viraintu oḻukutalai, car eṉṟu pāykiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
oḻukal nīṭcik karuttai yuṇarttum.
vārtal pōkal oḻukal mūṉṟum
nērpum neṭumaiyum ceyyum poruḷa  (tol. 800)
car - caram = nīṇṭa allatu nērāṉa maṇikkōvai allatu malarttoṭai.
carañcaramāyt toṅkukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
carakkoṉṟai = nēr malart toṭaiyākap pūkkum koṉṟai.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum ru eṉṉum coṟkum oḻuku, ōṭu eṉpaṉavē poruḷ.  (va.va. 144-145).
carukkam
carukkam - sarka (g)
carukkam - nūṟpirivu.

carukkarai
carukkarai - carkkarā
vaṭamoḻiyil caraḷ, ciṟukal, kūḻāṅkal, kaṟkaṇṭu eṉṟu poruḷ toṭarpu kāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.144).
caruvu
caruvu - caruku. carukutal = cāytal.
caruku - carukku. carukkutal = cāytal, vaḷaital, caṟukkutal.
carukku - carukkam = vaṭṭam, nūṟpirivu L. circum = vaṭṭam.
carukku - carukkaram - cakkaram = vaṭṭam, uruḷi, kuyavaṉ cakkaram, cakkarappaṭai, vaṭṭakkācu, cakkarappuḷ, mānilam, cekku.
cakkarap pāṭit teruvu  (periyapu. kaliyaṉā 5).
Gk kuklos, E cycle = cakkaram.
carukkaram - carukkarai - carkkarai - cakkarai = vaṭṭamāka vārkkap peṟṟa vellakkaṭṭi.
o.nō. vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa karuppukkaṭṭi.
carukkaram - (carukkāram) - cakkāram (mū.a.) = iṉiyatēmā. iṉṟum cakkaraikkuṭṭi eṉṟu oru māmpaḻap peyar vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
cakkāram - akkāram = carukkarai.
akkāramaṉṉār  (nālaṭi. 374)
akkāraṭalai, akkāravaṭicil eṉpaṉa carukkaraip poṅkal vakaikaḷ.
patanīrai (teḷivai) akkāranīr eṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
akkāram eṉṟu māṅkaṉikkum peyar. akkārakkaṉi naccumaṉār eṉṉum pulavarpeyar akkaṉiyāl vantatupōlum!
cakkaram - (cakku) - cekku = eṇṇeyāṭṭum vaṭṭamāṉa ural.
x.neh.: paru-peru, cattāṉ - cettāṉ.
cakku - cakkaṭam - cakkaṭā = kaṭṭai vaṇṭi.
te. cekkaṭā paṇṭi.
cakkaṭam - cakaṭam = cakkaram, vaṇṭi, tēr, vaṭṭil, muracu.
cakaṭam - cakaṭi = vaṇṭi. cakaṭikai = kaivaṇṭi.
cakaṭu = vaṇṭi. cakaṭu - cākāṭu = vaṇṭi. pīlipey cākāṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 476).
cakaṭu - cāṭu = vaṇṭi. kuṟuñcāṭ ṭuruḷai (perumpāṇ. 188).
cāṭu - cāṭukam = vaṇṭi.
cakaṭu - cakaṭai = vaṇṭi, muracu, amaṅkalac ciṟupaṟai.
cakaṭai - cakaṇṭai = muracu, paṟaippotu.
cakaṭam - cakōṭam = cakkarappuḷ, ataṉ vaṭivāṉa yāḻ.
cakōṭam = cakōram = cakkarappuḷ.
cakku - caṅku =- uḷ vaḷaintuḷḷa nantukkūṭu, valampuri, iṭampuri. purital - vaḷaital.
x.neh.: kōṭu = caṅku. curimukam = caṅku. vaḷai = caṅku. vāraṇam = caṅku.
caṅku - caṅkam = 1. caṅku. aṭutiraic caṅka mārppa (cīvaka. 701).
1. kaivaḷai. caṅkaṅ kaḻala (iṟai. 39 urai).
cakkaram mutal caṅku varai pala coṟkaḷ vaḷaivuk karuttaṭip paṭaiyil orē toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavātaliṉ, oruṅku kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
cakkara eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭiviṟku vaṭamoḻiyāḷarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa mūlam kru(cey) eṉpatē. mā.vi. akaramutali car (car) eṉṉum collai viṉākkuṟiyuṭaṉ kuṟittuḷḷatu. car = iyaṅku ivviraṇṭum, muṉṉatiṉ poruntāmaiyaiyum piṉṉatiṉ vaṉpuṇarpput taṉmaiyaiyum, aṟiñar kaṇṭu koḷka. cakaṭam - cakaṭa (ḷaÝ,va@).
mā. vi. a. “of doubtful derivation” eṉṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va. 135-137.)

cittam

citamparam - citampara (c)

tillaiyiṟ ciṟṟampalam pērampalam eṉa īrampalaṅkaḷuṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟṟampalamē iṟaivaṉ tirukkōyil.
